Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [269] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种分布无关、具有概率正确性保证的快速可扩展top-k选择算法。它通过单遍过滤：随机采样估计自适应阈值，将N个输入元素流式写入紧凑缓冲区，再在缓冲区上执行精确top-k，以至少1-ε的概率恢复真实top-k元素。实验显示比PyTorch topk和RadiK快1.5-10倍，并在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中展示了实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k选择方法效率低下：精确方法计算和内存开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式规则，在对抗性或重尾输入下性能退化。需要一种既快速又可扩展、且不依赖输入分布假设的top-k算法。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤流程：(1) 小规模随机采样估计自适应阈值；(2) 将N个输入元素一次性流式扫描到紧凑缓冲区中；(3) 在缓冲区上运行精确top-k例程。作者推导了正确性和缓冲区大小的高概率保证，并给出近似最优的采样规模以最小化N和k函数的开销。同时支持通过放宽召回目标（如恢复95%的真实top-k值）来实现精度-速度的权衡。

Result: 与高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK实现相比，Prof-K实现了1.5x-10x的加速，在大规模、小到中等k值的场景中收益最大。在训练BatchTopK稀疏自编码器时的实际应用中，显著降低了top-k选择在训练成本中的占比。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供了一种分布无关、具有概率保证的高效top-k算法，克服了现有方法对输入分布敏感和启发式脆弱的局限。其正确性保证不依赖于输入分布，对对抗性设置具有鲁棒性，并通过精度-速度权衡为用户提供了灵活的选择空间。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [270] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCoGen（Represent Conditions, then Generate）两阶段框架，将多模态条件表示与目标信号生成解耦，用于从异构、不规则缺失的临床数据和静态协变量中合成缺失的生理信号。在三个基准（AI-READI连续血糖监测、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV动脉血压生成）上，ReCoGen在全部十六个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中取得最佳下游效用，超过六种代表性条件生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续生理时间序列是现代临床监测的基础，但许多最具信息量的信号具有侵入性、成本高或对特定患者不可用。虽然条件生成可以通过联合记录的信号和常规临床变量合成缺失信号，但现有生成器围绕单一条件模态构建，无法很好地处理临床实践中常见的异构、不规则缺失的时间变异信号与静态协变量的组合。

Method: ReCoGen是一种两阶段框架：Stage I为每种模态训练一个掩码自编码器，将每个时间变异条件蒸馏为紧凑且抗缺失的token序列；Stage II训练一个流匹配（flow-matching）生成器，将这些token与静态条件融合以合成目标信号。关键设计包括：在冻结的逐模态编码器上使用可学习的交叉注意力，以及静态条件的双token+AdaLN通路。

Result: 在AI-READI（连续血糖监测）、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV（动脉血压生成）三个生理基准上，ReCoGen在全部十六个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中取得最佳下游效用，超越六种代表性条件生成器；在十三个设置中其效用达到或超过真实信号上测得的效用。消融实验表明增益来源于条件路径：可学习的交叉注意力与静态条件的双token+AdaLN路由。

Conclusion: ReCoGen能够将常规收集的信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的有效替代品，是向着更少侵入性、更低成本的连续临床监测迈出的一步。其成功关键在于将多模态条件表示与目标生成解耦，并通过掩码自编码器实现抗缺失的条件编码，以及流匹配生成器实现高质量信号合成。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [271] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通过随机低维重参数化训练/微调神经网络的可行性，提出了一个方向解析的二次主公式来预测达到低损失区域所需的潜在维度，并据此设计了Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN)框架，采用结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射，避免稠密随机映射的存储开销，同时开发了无矩阵曲率近似和免扫描维度选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络可通过冻结的随机映射将小潜在向量映射为完整参数更新，但如何确定使网络达到低损失区域所需的最小潜在搜索空间尺寸是一个开放且实际的问题。已有的高斯宽度二次界虽然提供了保守估计，但在方向敏感的位移场景下不够准确，需要更精细的理论工具。

Method: 1) 将已知的可达性转变重新表述为等价的锥形式，以极锥的统计维度为中心；2) 提出方向解析的二次主公式，从曲率谱和参考到解位移剖面预测随机切片残差；3) 推导自洽的各向同性取向预测器及保守半径特化的高斯宽度二次界；4) 提出RaMaN框架，使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生成高斯映射实现预测的潜在维度，将存储从O(dP)降至O(d)，并使用矩阵自由曲率近似和免扫描维度选择。

Result: 在受控二次曲线和神经曲率实验中，方向解析预测器密切追踪实测转变位置，并在位移方向重要的场景下优于忽略方向的近似方法。端到端实验在图像和语言模型上展示了尖锐的、依赖协议的训练转变现象。

Conclusion: 提出的方向解析预测器为随机低维重参数化中的可达性转变提供了更精确的理论刻画，RaMaN框架在保持预测能力的同時显著降低了计算和存储成本，为大规模神经网络的高效训练/微调提供了新途径。

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [272] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在有限简单无向图（带单个布尔节点特征）上，任意Σ-MPLang（Σ为逐步常数的激活函数集合，系数为任意实数）可表达的布尔查询严格少于ReLU-MPLang可表达的布尔查询，从而解决了“ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang更强大”这一开放问题，并隐含ReLU-GNN在布尔查询上严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决近期提出的开放问题：在布尔查询的语境下，ReLU-MPLang是否比trReLU-MPLang更具表达能力。这关系到图神经网络（GNN）在不同激活函数下的表达力层级划分。

Method: 在带单一布尔节点特征的有限简单无向图上，对任意由逐步常数（eventually constant）激活函数组成的集合Σ，证明Σ-MPLang可表达的布尔查询严格包含于ReLU-MPLang可表达的布尔查询之中。

Result: 证明了对于任意由逐步常数激活函数组成的集合Σ（系数为任意实数），Σ-MPLang的布尔查询表达能力严格弱于ReLU-MPLang；作为推论，ReLU-GNN在布尔特征图上的布尔查询表达能力严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。

Conclusion: ReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上严格强于任何由逐步常数激活函数（包括截断ReLU和恒等函数）构成的MPLang，这明确了ReLU激活在GNN布尔查询表达力上的独特优势。

Abstract: We prove that, on finite simple undirected graphs equipped with a single Boolean node feature, the Boolean queries expressible in $Σ$-MPLang, for any collection $Σ$ of eventually constant activation functions and with arbitrary real coefficients, form a strict subclass of the Boolean queries expressible in ReLU-MPLang. We thereby settle a recently posed open problem: whether ReLU-MPLang is more powerful than trReLU-MPLang when it comes to Boolean queries. In particular, this implies that ReLU-GNNs are strictly more expressive than {TrReLU,id}-GNNs with respect to Boolean queries on Boolean-featured graphs.

</details>


### [273] [Structure-preserving uncertainty quantification for GENERIC dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12624)
*Zequn He,Celia Reina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构保持认知神经网络（S-PENNs），一种用于具有硬架构约束的科学机器学习模型的不确定性量化通用框架。S-PENNs通过在预训练模型的约束组件上附加轻量级epinets来保持结构约束，确保每个采样实现都物理可用，并结合分裂共形预测提供有限样本边际覆盖保证。在GENERIC动力学上实例化，在三个数值示例（谐振子-热浴耦合、理想化学马达的ODE，及一维粘塑性模型的PDE）上验证，计算成本比深度集成降低1-3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构保持机器学习将物理结构直接嵌入模型架构，但此类硬约束模型的不确定性量化仍受限：标准UQ方法可能违反编码的物理允许性条件、需要架构修改或带来巨大计算成本。因此亟需一种既能保持物理约束又高效的不确定性量化框架。

Method: S-PENNs框架的核心思想是：对预训练模型的约束组件附加轻量级epinets（附加网络），通过构造保证每个采样实现都满足物理允许性条件。针对GENERIC动力学，该框架能保持热力学一致性（第一和第二定律）。此外，结合分裂共形预测作为事后校准方法，生成具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在三个数值示例（由ODE控制的谐振子-热浴耦合和理想化学马达，以及由PDE控制的一维粘塑性模型）上，S-PENNs产生热力学一致的随机实现和良好校准的预测区间，同时将计算成本相比深度集成降低约1-3个数量级。

Conclusion: S-PENNs为硬约束科学机器学习模型提供了一种高效、物理一致且满足统计覆盖保证的UQ框架。虽然本研究聚焦于GENERIC动力学，但该框架可推广到计算力学中具有硬或软约束的更广泛的科学机器学习模型。

Abstract: Structure-preserving machine learning embeds physical structure directly into model architectures, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such hard-constrained models remains limited because standard UQ methods may violate the encoded admissibility conditions, require architectural modifications, or impose substantial computational costs. In this work, we propose Structure-Preserving Epistemic Neural Networks (S-PENNs), a general framework for UQ in scientific machine learning models with hard architectural constraints, and instantiate it for GENERIC (General Equation for Non-Equilibrium Reversible-Irreversible Coupling) dynamics. S-PENNs preserve the structural constraints of a pretrained model by attaching lightweight epinets to its constrained components, ensuring that every sampled realization remains physically admissible by construction. When applied to GENERIC dynamics, such a proposed framework yields thermodynamically consistent rollouts that preserve the first and second laws. Furthermore, we combine S-PENNs with split conformal prediction as a post-hoc calibration method to produce prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees. We validate S-PENNs on three numerical examples: a harmonic oscillator coupled to a heat bath and an idealized chemical motor, both governed by ODEs, and a one-dimensional viscoplastic model governed by PDEs. Across all three examples, S-PENNs produce thermodynamically consistent stochastic realizations and well-calibrated prediction intervals while reducing the computational cost by about one to three orders of magnitude compared to deep ensembles. Although the present study focuses on GENERIC dynamics, S-PENNs can be extended more broadly to scientific machine learning models in computational mechanics with either hard or soft constraints.

</details>


### [274] [CAKE: Compiler-Agent Co-Design for Frontier Kernel Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12629)
*Zihao Ye,Yingyi Huang,Hongyi Jin,Bohan Hou,Junru Shao,Zhongming Yu,Jinqi Chen,Meghan Cowan,Shiyi Cao,Shanli Xing,Hanfeng Chen,Vinod Grover,Tianqi Chen,Luis Ceze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAKE是一个编译器-智能体协同设计的系统，通过硬件显式调度表示（CAKE IR）让GPU kernel智能体直接编写、验证和优化底层内核，在多个基准上超越现有基线并获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU kernel智能体和GPU编程语言长期分离发展：智能体将编译器视为固定黑盒，只能接收错误、正确性结果和时序反馈；而现有DSL要么隐藏关键调度决策，要么通过困难的布局抽象暴露它们。这导致专家级内核难以复现和自动化生成。

Method: CAKE提出编译器-智能体协同设计方法：智能体编写类型化、硬件显式的调度表示CAKE IR，该表示暴露warp角色、内存移动、同步和流水线调度，同时支持验证、成本建模和局部诊断。harness本身会演化——重复的失败会转化为验证规则、IR原语、模型校准和可复用优化策略。

Result: 在B200上匹配实现隐藏的Flash-KMeans干净起点测试中，80M token预算下最佳CAKE IR候选达到调优FlashML基线的1.144x，而直接CUDA/PTX仅0.928x。智能体生成的Kimi Delta Attention比官方FlashKDA获得2.05x几何平均加速并通过端到端服务验证。调度器支持的KNN和KMeans在400+形状上提升1.42x-2.12x性能，4个内核改动已作为上游PR提交。

Conclusion: CAKE通过在IR层面赋予智能体硬件级调度能力并让harness随经验演化，证明了智能体生成内核能够达到甚至超越人工调优的库实现，并将单一形状演化与库泛化和调度分离，为GPU内核自动生成提供了一个有效的协同设计范式。

Abstract: GPU kernel agents and GPU programming languages have advanced separately, leaving expert kernels difficult to reproduce. Agents usually treat the compiler as a fixed black box and receive only errors, correctness outcomes, and timing, while existing DSLs either hide critical scheduling decisions or expose them through difficult layout abstractions. We present CAKE, a compiler-agent co-design in which agents author CAKE IR, a typed, hardware-explicit schedule representation. CAKE exposes warp roles, memory movement, synchronization, and pipelines while supporting verification, cost modeling, and localized diagnostics. The harness itself evolves: recurring failures become verifier rules, IR primitives, model calibrations, and reusable optimization tactics. In matched implementation-hidden Flash-KMeans clean starts on B200, the best CAKE IR candidate at an 80-million-token budget runs at 1.144x the tuned FlashML baseline, compared with 0.928x for direct CUDA/PTX. Beyond this benchmark, agent-generated Kimi Delta Attention achieves a 2.05x geometric-mean speedup over official FlashKDA and passes end-to-end serving validation. Dispatcher-backed KNN and KMeans improve performance by 1.42x to 2.12x across more than 400 shapes, and four kernel changes are available as upstream PRs. CAKE targets NVIDIA GPUs from Ampere through Blackwell and separates single-shape evolution from library generalization and dispatch.

</details>


### [275] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件因果发现方法，将给定事件（如因果效应约束）下的因果图推断形式化为贝叶斯推断问题，并引入罕见事件估计技术（粒子驱动方法）来解决后验质量过小导致的计算难题，在合成图和Sachs蛋白质数据集上验证了方法的准确性和科学探索价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，还要能够解释观察到的或假设的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。然而，现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在事件后验质量很小时计算困难，无法有效地对满足特定事件约束的因果图和参数进行条件推断。

Method: 将条件因果发现构建为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是在给定事件（如因果效应约束）条件下估计因果图和参数的后验分布。为应对小后验质量事件的计算挑战，借鉴罕见事件估计技术，在联合图-参数空间中逐步将粒子种群推向约束区域，同时维持接近条件后验的样本分布。

Result: 在合成图上从大规模和小规模两个维度验证了方法的准确性；在Sachs蛋白质数据集上的案例研究表明，该方法能够提供通路级摘要，辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了小后验质量事件下条件因果发现的推断难题，兼具准确性和可扩展性，并能通过通路级总结支持实际科学研究中的假设探索与解释。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [276] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"(挑战下训练)框架，一种可执行证书机制，通过预声明的架构合法程序构造相同认证类别中的完整替代品并重新评估同一目标函数，用较低值候选作为可重放见证来下界估计检查点的经验全局最优性差距。该框架定义了资源索引的挑战性模量，在平方损失下可验证覆盖性，并证明了无覆盖时一阶ReLU训练器会收敛到非全局点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的平坦训练曲线无法区分网络是达到了全局最优、陷入局部最优、受表示能力限制，还是与训练器不匹配。现有方法缺乏可执行、可验证的机制来量化模型与全局最优之间的差距。

Method: 提出"Training Under Challenge"框架：(1)要求预声明架构合法的挑战程序构造完整替代模型并重新评估同一目标；(2)定义资源索引的挑战性功率模量以刻画可兼容的最大差距；(3)在平方损失下利用块下降算子实现可检查的覆盖性并推导均匀和实现残差界；(4)通过配对预测证书分离解码器利用不足与表示不足两种情况。

Result: 在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏问题中，8个内部挑战覆盖了240个审计输出方向，实现残差界与真实差距的比值在1.74-3.02之间。量化去噪研究展示了诊断、修复和当前状态的重认证流程。

Conclusion: 通过有限套件的测试只能说明套件相对性，全局差距结论需要单独论证的覆盖机制；没有覆盖机制时，存在收敛到非全局点但达到无穷多个精确条件头最优的一阶ReLU训练器反例。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [277] [Demand Transfer Estimation at Scale via Restricted Logit Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12680)
*Lakshya Garg,Deep Narayan Mishra,Swapnil Yadav,Haoan Wang,Sujal Alugubelli,Karthik Kumaran,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在大规模商品集（百万级以上商品）上高效估计需求转移（DT）系数的方法，用于商品需求预测与货架品类优化。与传统学习顾客选择模型的方法不同，该方法将独立需求预测与考虑商品间替代关系的调整相结合，从而避免为每个可能的商品组合单独进行需求预测。实验验证了在合理替代行为假设下，该方法的估计准确性及对需求预测的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统商品需求预测方法通过学习顾客选择模型来评估每个候选商品集的目标函数值，但面对包含大量品类的大规模商品库时效率低下，需要为每种可能的商品组合单独进行需求预测。因此需要一种能够在大规模商品库上高效预测需求、同时考虑商品间替代关系的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种计算需求转移（DT）系数的方法。DT系数表示当目标商品（顾客进店想购买的商品）从货架移除时，其需求转移到商品库中其他每个商品的百分比。该方法能够在百万级以上的大规模商品集上计算这些系数，并结合独立需求预测与基于DT系数的调整。

Result: 在数据及多个地点的历史交易数据实验中，当替代行为满足某些合理假设时，该方法能够准确估计潜在的需求转移系数，并在需求预测方面带来改进。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模商品库场景下的需求预测提供了一种计算高效的替代性方法，通过需求转移系数的估计有效捕捉了商品间的替代关系，在满足合理假设的前提下能够提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Item demand forecasting is an integral component of store assortment optimization. Existing literature focuses on learning a suitable customer choice model and using this model to determine the value of an objective function (i.e. expected demand) with respect to an assortment proposal. However, for large item universe with many categories, this approach can prove inefficient, needing a separate demand forecast for every possible item assortment. An alternate approach exists whereby we combine the efficiency of forecasting item demand independently, while at the same time applying adjustments to the independent forecasts that account for the relations between item demand and the availability of other similar items on the shelf.
  Central to this approach is the estimation of Demand Transfer (DT) coefficients. These DT coefficients represent the percent of a particular target item's (item that the customer walked in the store to buy) demand that is redirected to each other item in the universe should the target item be removed from the shelf. We introduce an approach that allows us to compute these DT coefficients on large item universes (assortments having 1 million+ items). Experiments on data as well as historical transaction data for multiple locations within categories demonstrate that when certain reasonable assumptions about substitution behavior are satisfied, our procedure is able to accurately estimate underlying DT coefficients and lead to improvements in demand forecasting.

</details>


### [278] [Finding the Needle in a Haystack: Test-Time Analog Circuit Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12687)
*Fin Amin,Sounak Dutta,Paul D. Franzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTARO，一种在线深度核贝叶斯优化框架，在搜索过程中持续调整模拟电路的表示，以对齐表示学习与优化目标，相比固定嵌入的BO和DKL分别平均降低15.2%和20.7%的遗憾AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于表示的贝叶斯优化方法通常在编码器训练后将电路嵌入视为固定不变，导致表示学习与优化目标之间存在不匹配：学习到的嵌入用于编码或重建电路结构，而非依据待优化的性能指标（FoM）组织。这种固定表示限制了搜索空间与优化目标的对齐。

Method: TTARO从预训练的电路嵌入出发，在搜索过程中联合学习非线性特征变换和高斯过程代理模型，利用已评估电路的FoM标签。每次新评估后，TTARO更新表示和代理模型，然后再选择下一个候选设计。

Result: 在40种编码器/核/采集函数设置组合下，TTARO相对于固定嵌入的BO平均降低15.2%的遗憾AUC，相对于DKL平均降低20.7%，在大多数设置中优于现有方法，最大降幅达46.7%。

Conclusion: 通过在搜索过程中持续将新观察到的FoM标签纳入表示学习，TTARO使搜索空间随BO进程逐步与优化目标对齐，有效提升了模拟电路拓扑搜索的样本效率。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a sample-efficient framework for analog circuit topology search, where evaluating each candidate topology can require costly simulation. However, representation-based BO methods typically treat circuit embeddings as fixed after encoder training. This creates a mismatch between representation learning and optimization: embeddings learned to encode or reconstruct circuit structure are not necessarily organized according to the figure of merit (FoM) being optimized. This paper introduces Test-Time Analog Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization (TTARO), an online deep-kernel BO framework that adapts circuit representations throughout the search process. Starting from pretrained circuit embeddings, TTARO jointly learns a nonlinear feature transformation and a Gaussian-process surrogate using the FoM labels of the circuits evaluated so far. Following each new evaluation, TTARO updates the representation and surrogate before selecting the next candidate. We compare TTARO with conventional Gaussian Process-based BO over fixed embeddings and with Deep Kernel Learning (DKL), which learns the representation only from the initial evaluated designs and keeps it fixed throughout the remainder of the search. By continually incorporating newly observed FoM labels into representation learning, TTARO aligns the search space with the optimization objective as BO progresses. In our experiments, TTARO reduces regret AUC by 15.2% on average relative to BO and by 20.7% relative to DKL across 40 encoder/kernel/acquisition settings, outperforming prior art in most settings with reductions as large as 46.7%.

</details>


### [279] [The Impact of Temporal Context Length and Encoding Strategies on Self-Supervised ECG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12695)
*Ahmed Sameh,Ramzi Al-Sharawi,Yogatheesan Varatharajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在Icentia11k单导联心电数据集上，系统比较了不同输入时间跨度（16秒至10分钟）和前馈表示方式（连续卷积patch嵌入vs.离散向量量化token）对自监督心电模型性能的影响，发现更长的时间上下文和连续的表示方式均能显著提升下游心律失常检测和患者级检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督心电模型通常仅使用几秒的心电信号和离散化的token序列进行训练，但尚不清楚这些选择是否会丢失心律推断和真实世界动态心电记录中纵向一致性所需的关键信息。

Method: 在Icentia11k单导联数据集上设计受控实验，变化两个维度：(i)输入时间范围（16秒、1分钟、5分钟、10分钟）和(ii)前端表示（连续卷积patch嵌入vs.固定向量量化token），同时保持Transformer骨干网络和训练协议不变。通过下游异常心律检测和患者级检索（探测跨会话稳定性）评估表示质量。

Result: 将时间上下文从16秒扩展到更长范围能获得更强的迁移能力和更高的检索准确率，其中5分钟和10分钟模型表现最佳，表明能更好地捕捉缓慢变化的心律动力学和个体特异性结构。在所有评估的时间范围内，连续patch嵌入均优于离散化token，表明量化可能丢弃临床相关的波形细节。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持心电基础模型应强调扩展上下文和连续编码器，以提升临床预测和基于相似性的应用性能。

Abstract: Self-supervised electrocardiogram (ECG) models are often trained on a few seconds of ECG signal and, increasingly, on discretized token sequences. It remains unclear whether these choices sacrifice information needed for rhythm inference and longitudinal consistency in real-world ambulatory recordings. We present a controlled study on the Icentia11k single-lead dataset that varies (i) the input horizon (16 seconds, 1 minute, 5 minutes, and 10 minutes) and (ii) the front-end representation (continuous convolutional patch embeddings vs. fixed vector-quantized tokens), while holding the Transformer backbone and training protocol constant. Representations are assessed by downstream abnormal rhythm detection and by patient-level retrieval that probes cross-session stability. Our results show that increasing temporal context beyond 16-second snapshots yields stronger transfer and higher retrieval accuracy, with the strongest performance achieved by the 5- and 10-minute models, indicating improved capture of slow-varying rhythm dynamics and individual-specific structure. Across all evaluated horizons, continuous patch embeddings outperform discretized tokens, suggesting that quantization can discard clinically relevant waveform detail. These findings motivate ECG foundation models that emphasize extended context and continuous encoders for clinical prediction and similarity-based applications. Our code and pretrained models are publicly available at https://github.com/muha-0/ecg-ssl-representation-learning.

</details>


### [280] [A Contract-Grade Verifier for LLM-Generated GPU Kernels, and a Native Blackwell Backward for the Gated-Linear-Recurrence Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12700)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有GPU内核生成系统的正确性评估存在严重漏洞：单一松散测试可能让错误内核通过。作者构建了一个包含12个对抗性门控的合同级验证器，对2,638个已被公开系统接受的内核进行审计，发现39.5%存在无法用容差解释的错误。同时，作者利用该验证器验证了自研的GDN（门控线性递归）家族原生Blackwell tcgen05训练反向内核的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU内核生成系统的正确性报告来自单一松散测试（固定shape上少数随机输入、输出接近参考即通过），这种测试会漏掉大量潜在的静默错误：NaN/Inf返回值错误、运行结果不稳定、shape变化时失效、fp16累计精度问题等。需要更严谨的验证机制来揭示真实正确率。

Method: 构建了合同级验证器，包含12个对抗性门控（adversarial gates），每个门控是正确内核必须满足的属性，其中多个为无容差（tolerance-free）约束。对外审计2,638个已被公开系统接受的机器生成内核；对内验证自研的GDN家族原生Blackwell tcgen05训练反向内核（含reverse-state阶段），以双精度oracle为参照。采用四种独立方式验证发现：7/7阳性对照、阈值校准扫描、与参考基准正确性代码98.5%一致、分层人工审计。

Result: 2,638个已通过公开系统测试的内核中，39.5%存在无法用容差解释的损坏，62.1%存在至少一项违规。标准测试接受了1,487个验证器拒绝的内核，而反向不一致仅14个。自研GDN内核通过独立验证，并成功训练五个家族成员。

Conclusion: 内核生成领域报告的正确性信号远弱于数字所暗示的；引入无容差的合同级验证可以弥合大部分差距。正确性验证需要从单一松散测试转向更严格、多维度、对抗性的契约验证体系。

Abstract: Systems that generate GPU kernels with language models report high correctness rates. Those rates come from a single loose test: run the kernel on a few random inputs at one fixed shape and accept it if the output is close to a reference. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. We build the instrument that checks correctness properly: a contract-grade verifier of twelve adversarial gates, each a property a correct kernel must satisfy, several of them tolerance-free, so no choice of threshold can explain a failure away. Aimed outward, the verifier audits 2,638 machine-generated kernels that a public system's own harness had already accepted as correct. It finds 39.5% broken beyond any tolerance argument and 62.1% carrying at least one violation. The field's standard test accepts 1,487 kernels the verifier rejects, against only 14 the other way. We defend the finding four independent ways: a 7/7 positive control, a threshold-calibration sweep, 98.5% agreement with the reference benchmark's own correctness code, and a stratified hand-audit. Aimed inward, the verifier judges a kernel of our own: the first native Blackwell tcgen05 training backward for the gated-linear-recurrence (GDN) family, including the reverse-state stage the field still runs on a fallback. We establish its correctness independently, against a double-precision oracle, and train five family members through it. The correctness signal behind reported progress in kernel generation is far weaker than the numbers suggest, and a set of tolerance-free contracts would close most of the gap.

</details>


### [281] [Federated Compositional Muon Optimizer for Matrix-Wise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12710)
*Wang Yan,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCoMuon优化器和其方差缩减变体FedCoMuon-VR，用于解决分布式矩阵级组合优化问题，在非独立同分布和非凸场景下证明了收敛性，并获得了比现有FedMuon算法更低的样本复杂度O(ε⁻³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在矩阵级AI模型中表现优异，但现有Muon及其变体方法不适用于层次化结构问题（如组合优化）。分布式组合优化需要专门设计的优化器，而现有方法在此场景下效果欠佳。

Method: 提出FedCoMuon优化器，基于组合梯度追踪（compositional gradient tracking）和正交化动量（orthogonalized momentum）技术；进一步提出方差缩减变体FedCoMuon-VR，采用基于动量的方差缩减技术降低样本复杂度。

Result: 在非独立同分布和非凸设置下分析了算法收敛性；证明FedCoMuon-VR的样本复杂度为O(ε⁻³)，优于现有FedMuon算法；在鲁棒联邦学习和任务分布式风险敏感元学习实验中获得最佳精度。

Conclusion: FedCoMuon和FedCoMuon-VR为分布式矩阵级组合优化提供了有效解决方案，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势，与现有组合优化基线相比具有竞争力。

Abstract: Muon, a more recently developed optimizer, is useful for matrix-wise models in AI areas. Although many works have studied Muon and its variants, these methods are still not particularly well-suited for hierarchical structured problems. To fill this gap, we propose an effective federated compositional Muon (FedCoMuon) optimizer to solve distributed matrix-wise compositional optimization problems. Specifically, our FedCoMuon optimizer builds on compositional gradient tracking and orthogonalized momentum. Moreover, we propose a variance reduced variant of FedCoMuon (FedCoMuon-VR) based on a momentum-based variance reduced technique. In theory, we analyze the convergence properties of our algorithms under the non-i.i.d. and non-convex settings. In particular, we prove that our FedCoMuon-VR obtains a lower sample complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary solution than the existing FedMuon algorithms. Extensive numerical experiments on robust federated learning and task-distributed risk-sensitive meta learning show that our proposed methods are competitive with existing compositional baselines and achieve the best reported accuracy in several settings.

</details>


### [282] [Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping (PRISM): naming-error dissociations in language models and post-stroke aphasia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12717)
*Xiang Guan,Roger D. Newman-Norlund,Yong Yang,Saeed Ahmadi,Regan Willis,Nadra Salman,Kalil Warren,Srihari Nelakuditi,Chris Rorden,Leonardo Bonilha,Julius Fridriksson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，将人类神经影像学中的减法分析（substract analysis）从生物大脑迁移至大语言模型（LLM），通过扰动种子作为受试者、层作为空间维度，对LLM内部组件进行可证伪、空间可解析的功能专门化测试。在LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B及213名慢性卒中失语症患者上并行运行结构匹配的分析，两侧均复现了稳健的语音偏向性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的机制可解释性缺乏空间可解析、可证伪的工具来检验内部组件是否专门化于不同的认知操作。现有方法难以直接验证功能专门化主张，需要借鉴成熟的人类神经影像学分析框架来填补这一空白。

Method: PRISM基于Brain-LLM Unified Model (BLUM)框架，采用减法分析逻辑：将7个临床费城命名测试类别进行两两差减，将每个扰动种子视为组分析中的受试者，沿层轴使用无阈值簇增强（TFCE）。在人类侧，对213名慢性卒中后失语症患者运行基于相关差值的病灶-症状映射（correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping）。两侧在设计上匹配受试者维度（种子vs患者）、空间维度（层vs图谱分割皮层）和阈值化，但对比算子不同：LLM使用受试者内误差比例差，皮层使用受试者间相关差值。两侧均在保持集上复现。

Result: 两个基底（LLM和人类皮层）均恢复了稳健的语音偏向性分离——LLM出现深层簇，皮层出现额-外侧裂周围皮质簇，且均在保持集上复现。语义偏向方向在两侧均呈现一致符号但非显著的趋势。PRISM提供了可证伪的、空间可解析的针对转换器语言模型功能专门化主张的测试。

Conclusion: PRISM成功将人类神经影像学的减法分析框架适配到转换器语言模型，能在层维度上对功能专门化主张进行可证伪的检验。LLM与人类大脑在语音/语义分离模式上的跨基底一致性支持了BLUM的统一模型假设。确认性的ROI层面干预（PRISM阶段3）——即最具因果机制强度主张的验证——留待后续工作。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of large language models lacks spatially resolved, falsifiable tools for testing whether internal components are specialized for distinct cognitive operations. We adapt subtraction analysis, the standard framework of human neuroimaging, from biological brains to perturbed transformers, and apply the same logic to both substrates in parallel. Building on the Brain-LLM Unified Model (BLUM), which showed that layer-perturbed LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B error profiles match the lesion patterns of aphasic patients, we develop PRISM (Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping). PRISM maps the seven clinical Philadelphia Naming Test categories, subtracts error classes pairwise, and treats each perturbation seed as a subject in a group analysis with threshold-free cluster enhancement along the layer axis. We run a structurally matched analysis on 213 chronic post-stroke aphasia patients using correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping, and replicate both sides on held-out splits. The designs match in subject dimension (seeds, patients), spatial dimension (layers, atlas-parcellated cortex) and thresholding, but the contrast operator differs: a within-subject error-proportion difference for the LLM, a between-subject correlation difference for the cortex. Both substrates recover a robust phonemic-favoring dissociation, a deep layer cluster and a frontal-perisylvian cortical cluster, both replicating; the semantic-favoring direction is a consistently signed but non-significant trend on both. PRISM thus gives a falsifiable, spatially resolved test of functional-specialization claims in transformer language models. A confirmatory ROI-level intervention (PRISM Stage 3) licensing the strongest causal-mechanism claim is left to subsequent work.

</details>


### [283] [MAG: MAnifold Guided Semi-Supervised Multi-modal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12724)
*Zirui Cheng,Xun Xu,Tiankai Chen,Fady Rezk,Bowen Zheng,Xiaodong Shi,Shijie Li,Kangkang Lu,Bharadwaj Veeravalli,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAG框架，利用无标注多模态数据改进少样本多模态上下文学习中的演示选择，通过图上的半监督传播策略，在标签稀缺场景下显著超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的少样本上下文学习对所选演示样本的质量和覆盖度高度敏感，而大量无标注多模态数据虽然丰富，却尚未被有效利用于上下文学习中的演示选择。

Method: MAG将演示选择建模为多模态图上的半监督传播问题，采用两阶段策略：阶段一通过相关性分数传播筛选出高影响力的无标注样本用于伪标注，降低MLLM推理成本；阶段二利用多模态相关性选择最终演示。研究发现文本表示更适合相关性传播，而视觉和文本模态共同决定高质量演示选择。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上的实验表明，MAG在标签稀缺场景下持续优于强基线，并在有限的伪标注预算下取得显著提升。

Conclusion: MAG验证了利用无标注多模态数据改进ICL演示选择的有效性，揭示了文本表示在相关性传播中的主导作用以及多模态融合在最终选择中的必要性，为半监督多模态上下文学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Few-shot in-context learning (ICL) with multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) enables task adaptation without parameter updates, but its performance is highly sensitive to the quality and coverage of the selected demonstrations. While unlabeled multi-modal data is abundant, it remains elusive how to exploit them for ICL. We propose MAG (MAnifold-Guided semi-supervised in-context demonstra- tion selection), an efficient framework that leverages unlabeled data to improve multi-modal ICL. MAG formulates demonstration selection as a semi-supervised propagation problem on a multi-modal graph and adopts a two-stage strategy: (i) relevance score propagation identifies a compact set of high-impact unlabeled samples for pseudo-labeling, reducing MLLM inference cost; (ii) multi-modal relevance is used to select the final demonstrations. We show that textual represen- tations are more effective for relevance propagation, while both visual and textual modalities are crucial for high-quality demonstration selection. Experiments on eight multi-modal benchmarks demonstrate that MAG consistently outperforms strong baselines in label-scarce regimes, achieving significant gains with a limited pseudo-labeling budget.

</details>


### [284] [A Cloud-Edge System for Multimodal Clinical Screening in Resource-Constrained Rural Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12745)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Luis Filipe Nakayama,Michael G. Morley,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种云-边缘协同架构，将轻量级边缘模型与云端大语言模型结合，在带宽受限、算力有限的农村医疗场景中实现高效的多模态临床诊断，在20个临床案例和三种网络条件下达到98-99%的工具召回率、92-96%的精确率，并保持25-35秒的带宽无关延迟，同时降低4-15倍token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI虽已达到专科级诊断准确率，但在带宽稀缺、计算受限的资源匮乏农村环境中难以部署。现有云端方案在低带宽环境下不可行，而边缘模型能力有限，需要一种协同架构来弥合这一鸿沟，实现多模态数据的高效集成与临床决策支持。

Method: 提出云-边缘协同架构：1) 边缘端部署轻量级领域专用模型，将原始医疗数据转化为紧凑的结构化输出；2) 云端LLM综合这些输出生成临床摘要；3) 基于LLM的编排器根据患者上下文动态选择诊断工具，确保全面的模态覆盖同时避免处理无关输入。在500kbps-5Mbps三种模拟网络配置下，对20个涵盖心脏、产科、创伤和筛查场景的多模态临床案例进行评估。

Result: 该混合系统实现98-99%的诊断工具召回率和92-96%的精确率；临床准确性与云端基线相当或更优；在25-35秒内保持与带宽无关的延迟；token成本降低4-15倍。

Conclusion: 架构设计在实现高效多模态集成和改善事实性接地方面起关键作用，在部署约束下相比纯云端方法具有显著优势，为资源受限环境下的医疗AI部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Medical AI has demonstrated specialist-level diagnostic accuracy, yet these capabilities remain largely inaccessible in resource-constrained rural settings where bandwidth is scarce, compute is limited, and clinical decision-making requires integrating heterogeneous modalities. We introduce a cloud--edge collaborative architecture that addresses these constraints: lightweight, domain-specific models on the edge transform raw medical data into compact structured outputs, while a cloud LLM synthesizes these outputs into clinical summaries. An LLM-based orchestrator dynamically selects diagnostic tools based on patient context, promoting comprehensive modality coverage without processing irrelevant inputs. We evaluate on 20 multimodal clinical cases spanning cardiac, obstetric, trauma, and screening scenarios under three simulated network profiles (500,kbps--5,Mbps). The hybrid system achieves 98--99% diagnostic tool recall with 92--96% precision, matches or exceeds cloud-only baselines on clinical accuracy, and maintains bandwidth-invariant latency (25--35,s) at 4--15x lower token cost. These results highlight the role of architectural design in enabling efficient multimodal integration and improving factual grounding compared to cloud-only approaches under deployment constraints.

</details>


### [285] [Decentralized Multi-Player Q-Learning in Episodic Markov Decision Processes with Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12753)
*Larissa Xu,King Bi,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究回合制表格马尔可夫决策过程中的去中心化多智能体强化学习，在三种信息不对称形式下（A.未观测动作+共同奖励；B.观测动作+独立奖励；C.未观测动作+独立奖励）提出算法，实现了与集中式单智能体Q-learning相同的遗憾界（至多相差对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多智能体系统中，智能体之间往往无法通信且存在信息不对称（如无法观测他人动作、奖励相互独立等）。本文旨在回答：在去中心化且信息受限的条件下，多智能体强化学习能否达到与集中式学习相同的效率？

Method: 针对问题A和B提出mQ-learning和mQ-learning-intervals算法，实现Õ(√(H⁴·S·A_joint·T))遗憾界；针对问题C提出mEXC和mEXC-Bellman两阶段探索-然后-承诺算法，实现Õ(H·(S·A_joint)^{1/3}·T^{2/3})遗憾界。算法允许玩家在学习前约定协议但不能通信。

Result: 问题A和B达到Õ(√(H⁴S A_joint T))遗憾，问题C达到Õ(H(SA_joint)^{1/3}T^{2/3})遗憾。与集中式联合动作基准相比，去中心化学习在信息不对称下匹配了单智能体Q-learning的遗憾率（jin2018q）直至对数因子。由于A_joint随玩家数M呈指数增长，这些界在小M或小动作集时最有意义。

Conclusion: 去中心化多智能体强化学习在信息不对称条件下可以匹敌集中式基准的性能，揭示了共享协议与预先协调足以弥补通信缺失带来的学习效率损失。

Abstract: We study decentralized multi-player reinforcement learning in episodic tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs) under three forms of information asymmetry: (A) unobserved actions with common rewards, (B) observed actions with independent rewards, and (C) unobserved actions with independent rewards. Players cannot communicate during learning but may agree on a protocol a priori. For Problems A and B we propose \texttt{mQ-learning} and \texttt{mQ-learning-intervals}, achieving $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{H^4 S A_{\text{joint}}\, T})$ regret, where $H$ is the horizon, $S$ the state count, $T = KH$ the total steps, and $A_{\text{joint}} = \prod_{i=1}^M |\mathcal{A}_i|$ the joint action space across $M$ players. For Problem C we give \texttt{mEXC} and \texttt{mEXC-Bellman}, two-phase explore-then-commit algorithms with regret $\tilde{O}(H (S A_{\text{joint}})^{1/3} T^{2/3})$. Against the centralized joint-action benchmark, decentralized learning under information asymmetry matches the single-agent Q-learning rate of \cite{jin2018q} up to logarithmic factors. Because $A_{\text{joint}}$ grows exponentially in $M$, the bounds are most meaningful for small $M$ or small per-player action sets.

</details>


### [286] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12764)
*Haoze Wu,Chuqiao Kuang,Tianyi Zhuang,Xiaoguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO（Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization），通过基于证据锚点的逐步自蒸馏，将教师-学生间的分布分歧转化为GRPO中的逐步优势权重，解决深度搜索代理中奖励过稀疏的信用分配问题。在Qwen3-8B上，SSPO在BrowseComp、GAIA和FRAMES三个基准上一致优于GRPO，仅增加约5%的额外计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理的轨迹通常跨越数十步，然而标准强化学习仅提供单一的结果奖励，信号过于稀疏，难以进行有效的信用分配。虽然自监督蒸馏（OPSD）可以利用模型自身logits作为稠密的token级教师信号，但将其扩展到搜索代理时存在根本矛盾：教师拥有特权信息（如正确答案），其分布与学生的基于探索的推理分布存在系统性差异，朴素蒸馏会使学生继承这种信息不对称，而非学习更好的搜索策略。

Method: 本文通过两项贡献解决上述矛盾：（1）构建证据锚点（Evidence Anchors）——从网络中提取的简洁、步骤级的证据片段，作为特权信息捕捉关键推理步骤而不暴露完整答案路径；（2）提出逐步级自蒸馏策略优化（SSPO）——将教师-学生的分歧转化为GRPO中的逐步优势权重，且仅应用于错误的轨迹。该设计将"更新什么"与"更新多少"解耦：结果奖励决定策略变化方向，教师调节每一步的变化幅度；正确轨迹保持不变以保留多样性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，SSPO在BrowseComp、GAIA和FRAMES三个基准上一致优于GRPO，性能超越或匹配使用两倍梯度步数训练的GRPO，每步仅增加约5%的额外开销（来源于单次额外前向传播）。

Conclusion: 通过证据锚点提供合适的特权信息，并利用逐步自蒸馏将教师分歧转化为优势权重，SSPO在不显著增加计算开销的前提下有效解决了搜索代理场景下奖励稀疏带来的信用分配问题，证明了"结果奖励定方向、教师信号调幅度"这一解耦设计在深度搜索场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Deep search agents operate over trajectories spanning dozens of steps, yet standard reinforcement learning provides only a single outcome reward per trajectory, which is far too sparse for effective credit assignment. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) addresses this by using the model's own logits as dense token-level teachers, but extending it to search agents introduces a fundamental tension: the teacher, having access to privileged information such as the correct answer, produces a distribution that differs systematically from the student's exploration-based reasoning, and naive distillation causes the student to inherit this information asymmetry rather than learn better search strategies. We resolve this tension through two contributions. First, we construct Evidence Anchors, which are concise, step-level evidence snippets extracted from the web, as privileged information that captures key reasoning steps without revealing the entire answer path. Second, we propose Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization (SSPO), which converts teacher-student disagreement into step-level advantage weights within GRPO, applied exclusively to incorrect trajectories. This design decouples what to update from how much to update: the outcome reward determines the direction of policy change, while the teacher modulates its magnitude at each step. Correct trajectories are left untouched, preserving their diversity. On Qwen3-8B, SSPO consistently outperforms GRPO across BrowseComp, GAIA, and FRAMES, surpassing or matching GRPO trained with twice as many gradient steps while adding only about 5 percent overhead per step from a single additional forward pass.

</details>


### [287] [CoMedBench: A Multi-Source Benchmark of Synthetic Medical Data Fidelity and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12805)
*Akanta Das,Al Amin Farhad,Mrinmoy Sarkar Anto,David Rehkopf,Ayin Vala,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoMedBench是一个可复现的临床合成数据基准，在统一框架下评估多种生成器在37个数据集-任务组合（20个静态表格+17个时序ICU任务）上的表现，涵盖MIMIC-III/IV、eICU等七个数据源。结果显示合成训练数据在表格任务中保留90.6-97.3%的下游信号（AUROC），时序ICU任务更难且对生成器敏感（AUROC 81.6%，AUPRC仅64.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据获取受隐私法规、机构审查和数据使用协议限制，而先前合成数据研究多局限于单一生成器、单一数据集或窄下游任务，难以判断合成数据何时能支持模型开发、何时会丢失任务关键信号，缺乏系统性、可复现的评估基准。

Method: 构建CoMedBench基准，在统一的临床有效性框架和共享训练/评估引擎下，对一系列生成器进行横向评估，跨越静态表格（20个任务）和时序ICU（17个任务）两类模态，使用七个公开数据源，通过真实/合成数据交叉训练-测试的协议同时评估统计保真度和任务效用。

Result: 静态表格任务中，参考生成器CoMed-CTGAN平均AUROC效用为90.6%，最强生成器CoMed-TVAE达到97.3%；时序ICU任务更具挑战，CoMed-CTGAN保留81.6% AUROC但仅64.0% AUPRC，而CoMed-TVAE仍保留约95% AUROC。

Conclusion: 合成数据在多数下游临床任务中能保留大部分信号，尤其表格任务表现良好；但时序ICU任务对生成器选择高度敏感，且在不平衡敏感指标（AUPRC）下性能显著退化，表明时序、类别不平衡场景仍是合成数据的薄弱环节。

Abstract: Access to clinical data is essential for developing reliable healthcare machine learning systems, but direct use of electronic health records is constrained by privacy regulation, institutional review, data-use agreements, and the risk of re-identification. Synthetic data promises a practical alternative: it can preserve useful statistical and clinical structure while reducing exposure of sensitive patient records. Prior studies often evaluate a single generator, one dataset, or a narrow downstream task, making it difficult to know when synthetic data can support model development and when it fails to preserve task-critical signal. We introduce CoMedBench, a reproducible benchmark that evaluates a family of generators under a common clinical-validity framework and one shared training and evaluation engine, spanning static tabular and temporal downstream tasks on established critical-care datasets. In total the benchmark spans 37 dataset-task pairs across two modalities consists of 20 static tabular and 17 temporal ICU time-series-drawn from seven public data sources: three intensive-care databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU) together with the UCI Machine Learning Repository, the CDC BRFSS diabetes cohort (2015), NHANES (1999-2014), and the pycox survival datasets (GBSG and METABRIC). The benchmark evaluates both statistical fidelity and task utility by comparing models trained and tested across real and synthetic data. In these settings, synthetic training data preserves most of the downstream signal: on tabular tasks the reference generator CoMed-CTGAN retains a mean AUROC utility (the synthetic-to-real performance ratio) of 90.6%, rising to 97.3% for the strongest generator, CoMed-TVAE. Temporal ICU tasks are harder and more generator-sensitive: CoMed-CTGAN retains 81.6% (AUROC) and only 64.0% under the imbalance-sensitive AUPRC, whereas CoMed-TVAE still retains ~95% (AUROC).

</details>


### [288] [HiRoute: Hierarchical Routed Prompt Tuning for Safety Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12821)
*Fangzhou Chen,Shiji Zhao,Mengyang Wang,Qihui Zhu,Ranjie Duan,Maoxun Yuan,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiRoute是一个输入自适应的分层提示微调框架，通过轻量级路由器和共享/细粒度提示专家的组合，在冻结的大语言模型上实现跨类别的安全对齐，兼顾安全性与响应有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效的安全对齐方法（如基于提示微调的方法）通常依赖单一全局提示或外部选定的提示模块，这种静态设计难以在保持跨类别安全边界的同时，针对具体风险生成建设性响应并避免对良性输入的过度拒绝。

Method: HiRoute采用分层提示微调框架：首先在冻结LLM的表示上训练轻量级分层路由器，联合检测有害意图和预测多标签风险分数；随后冻结骨干模型和路由器，通过交替梯度更新的偏好优化学习共享粗粒度提示和一组细粒度提示专家（连续嵌入）。推理时，良性输入绕过安全分支，风险输入则使用共享提示与路由器加权的风险特定提示专家混合处理。

Result: 在三个指令微调模型上的实验表明，HiRoute在多个安全基准上实现了高安全率，同时保持了安全响应的有用性，减少了过度拒绝，并在通用任务上保持了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: HiRoute通过分离类别无关的安全控制与类别特定的响应指导，有效地解决了静态提示设计在安全对齐中的局限性，实现了安全性与有用性的更好权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to harmful requests and jailbreak attacks. Parameter-efficient safety alignment methods based on prompt tuning typically rely on a single global prompt or externally selected prompt modules. Such static designs struggle to maintain a cross-category safety boundary while generating constructive responses tailored to specific risks and avoiding over-refusal of benign inputs. To address these limitations, we propose HiRoute, an input-adaptive hierarchical prompt-tuning framework that separates category-agnostic safety control from category-specific response guidance. HiRoute first trains a lightweight hierarchical router on representations extracted from a frozen LLM to jointly detect harmful intent and predict multi-label risk scores. It then freezes both the backbone model and the router and uses preference optimization with alternating gradient updates to learn a shared coarse-grained prompt and a set of fine-grained prompt experts as continuous embeddings. At inference time, benign inputs bypass the safety branch, whereas risky inputs are processed using the shared prompt together with a router-weighted mixture of risk-specific prompt experts. Experiments across three instruction-tuned models show that HiRoute achieves high safety rates across multiple safety benchmarks while preserving safe-response helpfulness, reducing over-refusal, and maintaining competitive performance on general-purpose tasks.

</details>


### [289] [Fast A/B/n Testing: Exact Multi-Policy Comparison via Tree-Coupled Feedback Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12831)
*Yuxiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tree-Coupled A/B Testing (TCAB)，一种精确反馈共享设计，用于任意历史依赖的上下文bandit策略对比。通过在每轮用可预测树连接策略历史并最大化耦合父-子上下文-动作律、在匹配边缘组件内共享一次奖励，使每个策略保持独立的有限时域轨迹律，同时将奖励查询次数从JT降为T+累积树边总变差（期望），在次线性伪遗憾条件下达到T+o(T)，显著改善成本-精度前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需要同时对比大量自适应决策策略（排序系统、推荐算法、定价规则、语言模型代理等），但每次带奖励的交互都可能有成本或风险。传统A/B/n设计给每个策略独立的T轮轨迹，总奖励查询为JT次，成本过高。

Method: TCAB在每个回合用一棵可预测树连接当前所有策略的历史；对每个父-子上下文-动作律执行最大耦合；在匹配树边的每个连通分量内共享一个奖励。定义D_{e,t}为第t轮树边e上的不匹配指示，奖励查询数满足路径恒等式N(T)=T+ΣD_{e,t}。文章还分析了该设计的条件最优性（在选定树上的精确边局部设计中），并提出当前轮最小生成树的即时最优性。对固定J，若每个策略具有次线性伪遗憾且oracle动作几乎必然唯一，则E[N(T)]=T+o(T)。同时给出成对策略对比的有限样本方差界。

Result: 实验覆盖奖励模型评估、多选题语言模型评估和自适应搜索策略，显示TCAB在成本-精度前沿上有显著改进；理论上证明了路径级奖励查询恒等式、条件最优性、即时最优性，以及在次线性伪遗憾条件下的T+o(T)成本上界。

Conclusion: TCAB提供了一种通用的、条件最优的反馈共享机制，使多个历史依赖策略在不破坏各自独立轨迹分布的前提下共享奖励，将测试成本从JT大幅降至约T。该设计为在线策略比较提供了一条低成本高精度的新路径，尤其适用于奖励获取昂贵的场景（如真实用户交互、语言模型评估）。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly compare many adaptive decision policies---ranking systems, recommendation algorithms, pricing rules, and language-model agents---while each reward-bearing interaction can be costly or risky. A direct A/B/n design gives each of $J$ policies its own horizon-$T$ trajectory and therefore uses $JT$ outcomes. We introduce Tree-Coupled A/B Testing (\TCAB), an exact feedback-sharing design for arbitrary history-dependent contextual-bandit policies. At each round, a predictable tree connects the current policy histories; every parent--child context--action law is maximally coupled, and one reward is shared within each component of matched tree edges. Every policy retains exactly its standalone finite-horizon trajectory law, even though the policies are deliberately dependent. If $D_{e,t}$ records a mismatch on tree edge $e$ at round $t$, the number of reward queries satisfies the pathwise identity $N(T)=T+\sum_{t,e}D_{e,t}$ and hence equals $T$ plus cumulative tree-edge total variation in expectation. This cost is conditionally optimal among exact edge-local designs on the selected tree, and a current-round minimum-spanning tree is myopically optimal among tree designs. For fixed $J$, sublinear pseudo-regret of every policy and almost-sure uniqueness of the oracle action imply $\mathbb{E}[N(T)]=T+o(T)$, versus $JT$ for independent runs. We also obtain finite-sample variance bounds for pairwise policy contrasts. Experiments on reward-model evaluation, multiple-choice language-model evaluation, and adaptive search policies demonstrate substantial improvements in the cost--precision frontier.

</details>


### [290] [A Compositional Theory of Curvature in Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12869)
*Hrithik Suresh,Sahil Sidheekh,Shelar Parth Vijay,Yasir Z,Sriraam Natarajan,Narayanan Chatapuram Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究概率电路（PCs）的损失曲面曲率，证明每个求和节点的Hessian迹贡献可精确分解为电路流量与局部锐度项的乘积，并据此提出自适应的锐度感知正则化方法，在保留闭式EM更新的同时恢复全局正则化牺牲的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCs作为支持精确推理的生成模型，其对数似然的Hessian迹可精确计算，但现有工作将其作为全局正则化项存在缺陷——PC的曲率本质上是组合式的，全局锐度正则化具有深度偏差且可能导致欠拟合。因此需要更精确地理解PC曲率的结构并设计针对性的正则化方法。

Method: 1) 理论证明：证明每个求和节点对Hessian迹的贡献可精确因子化为电路流量（衡量节点使用频率）与局部锐度项（由节点输出分布决定）的乘积；2) 基于此分解揭示全局锐度正则化深度偏差的根源；3) 提出自适应锐度感知正则化器，按节点固有局部曲率进行惩罚，并保持闭式EM更新可行。

Result: 实证结果表明，所提出的针对性正则化方法能够恢复全局正则化所牺牲的泛化性能，同时保留锐度感知学习的鲁棒性和其他优势。

Conclusion: PC的Hessian迹具有内在的组合结构，将锐度作为全局正则化是错误设定的。通过将锐度惩罚分解到各个节点并根据局部曲率自适应调整，可以在享受锐度感知学习益处的同时避免全局正则化的欠拟合与深度偏差问题。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are generative models that support exact inference and, unlike deep neural networks, admit an exact and tractable measure of loss-surface curvature: the trace of the Hessian of the log-likelihood. Recent work regularizes this trace globally to bias learning toward flatter, better generalizing optima. We show that treating sharpness as a global regularizer can be misspecified for PCs, whose curvature is inherently compositional. We prove that each sum node's contribution to the Hessian trace factorizes exactly into its circuit flow, which measures how heavily the node is used, and a local sharpness term determined by its output distribution. This decomposition provides insights into why global sharpness regularization is depth biased and can lead to underfitting. Building on it, we introduce an adaptive sharpness aware regularizer that penalizes nodes based on intrinsic local curvature and preserves closed form EM updates. We also show that empirically, this targeted regularization recovers the generalization that global regularization sacrifices while retaining the robustness and benefits of sharpness aware learning.

</details>


### [291] [Sustaining Plasticity via Learnable Wavelet Activations in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12874)
*Zeyang Zhang,Tieliang Gong,Junyan Lu,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可学习的小波激活函数，将激活函数分解为低频与高频分量以对抗频谱偏差，并结合动态小波注入与正则化策略，在持续学习中同时保持可塑性与稳定性，理论上有严格数学保证，实验上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中可塑性丧失严重阻碍了顺序任务的获取。现有固定形式的激活函数存在对低频变化的内在频谱偏差，而可学习的变体则允许无约束更新从而诱发灾难性遗忘，二者均无法兼顾可塑性与稳定性。

Method: 提出可学习的小波激活函数，将激活分解为低频和高频组件显式对抗频谱偏差；采用动态小波注入机制自适应增强新任务的可塑性；结合正则化策略保证旧知识稳定性。理论上证明混合小波架构对高效L2逼近的结构必要性，论证解耦学习率机制可恢复高频信息的网络可塑性，并形式化推导损失驱动注入触发机制。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准上，该方法保持优越的可训练性和泛化能力，达到最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过将小波理论与可学习激活设计相结合，本文在持续学习中成功实现了可塑性与稳定性的平衡，为后续激活函数设计提供了新的理论框架与实用方案。

Abstract: Plasticity loss has emerged as a critical challenge in continual learning that significantly hinders the acquisition of sequential tasks. While optimizing activation designs offers a potential solution, current fixed-form functions suffer from an inherent spectral bias towards low-frequency variations, whereas learnable variants permit unconstrained updates that induce catastrophic forgetting. To address these limitations, we propose a novel learnable wavelet activation that decomposes the activation function into low-frequency and high-frequency components to explicitly counter spectral bias. Furthermore, we employ dynamic wavelet injection to adaptively enhance plasticity for new tasks, alongside a regularization strategy to ensure the stability of previous learned knowledge. Theoretically, we provide rigorous mathematical guarantees for the proposed framework, proving the structural necessity of the hybrid wavelet architecture for efficient $L^2$ approximation and demonstrating that the decoupled learning rate mechanism successfully restores network plasticity for high-frequency information. Additionally, we provide a formal derivation of the loss-driven injection trigger mechanism to precisely guide the injection. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains superior trainability and generalization throughout the learning process and achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [292] [Robust data-driven discovery of fractional differential equations via weak formulations and Pareto-based subset selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12879)
*Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weak-Pareto方法，结合伴随一致的弱形式与Pareto子集选择，从噪声数据中发现分数阶偏微分方程。弱形式将分数算子从测量场转移到平滑测试函数，抑制噪声放大；利用岭回归与分支感知差分进化搜索连续分数阶，并在验证误差-复杂度拐点处选择支撑集。在多种基准上优于强形式方法和神经网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程描述非局部动力学，但从含噪数据中发现它们十分困难：分数阶微分会放大高频测量噪声，且导数阶次未知。现有方法对噪声敏感，需要一种能同时处理噪声放大和未知分数阶的方程发现框架。

Method: 1) 采用伴随一致的弱公式化处理分数阶项，将分数算子从测量场转移到光滑测试函数，以平滑积分替代对噪声敏感的点微分；2) 对线性右端项，噪声抑制效果显著；对非线性项部分有效；3) 在分支感知的差分进化搜索中对分数阶进行连续优化，系数通过岭回归拟合；4) 在验证误差-复杂度拐点处选择支撑集大小（Pareto子集选择）。

Result: 固定线性右端项的弱特征方差在网格细化下趋于零，而点态分数特征的噪声放大随导数阶次增加。在分数阶对流-扩散、反应-扩散和Burgers基准上均能恢复简洁结构。在受控对流-扩散和Burgers比较中，Weak-Pareto在所有测试的乘性噪声水平下保持正确支撑集，而强形式方法在引入噪声后基本失效；此优势在加性高斯噪声下依然保持。消融实验表明弱库驱动了噪声鲁棒性，连续阶次Pareto搜索避免了稠密固定字典的支撑选择失败。在对流-扩散基准上，Weak-Pareto比神经基线获得更一致的算子恢复和显著更低的运行时间。

Conclusion: Weak-Pareto通过弱形式-伴随转移与Pareto子集选择的组合，有效解决了分数阶PDE从噪声数据发现的噪声放大与阶次未知两大挑战，在多个基准上展现优越的鲁棒性、一致性和计算效率。

Abstract: Fractional partial differential equations describe nonlocal dynamics, but discovering them from noisy data is difficult because fractional differentiation amplifies high-frequency measurement noise and the derivative orders are unknown. We propose Weak-Pareto, which combines an adjoint-consistent weak formulation of fractional terms with Pareto-based subset selection over discrete term types and continuous fractional orders. For linear right-hand-side terms, the adjoint transfers fractional operators from measured fields to smooth test functions, replacing noise-sensitive pointwise differentiation with smoothing integration; for nonlinear terms, the noise-suppression effect is partial yet useful. Coefficients are fitted by ridge regression within a branch-aware differential-evolution search over the orders. The support size is then selected at the validation-error-complexity elbow. We show that the variance of fixed linear right-hand-side weak features vanishes under grid refinement, whereas noise amplification in pointwise fractional features increases with derivative order. Across fractional advection-diffusion, reaction-diffusion, and Burgers benchmarks, Weak-Pareto recovers parsimonious structures from clean and noisy measurements. In controlled advection-diffusion and Burgers comparisons, it retains the correct support at every tested multiplicative-noise level, whereas the unregularised strong-form counterpart largely fails once noise is introduced; this advantage persists under additive Gaussian noise. Ablations show that the weak library drives noise robustness and that continuous-order Pareto search avoids the support-selection failure of a dense fixed dictionary. On the advection-diffusion benchmark, Weak-Pareto yields more consistent operator recovery and substantially lower measured runtime than a contemporary neural baseline.

</details>


### [293] [Adaptive $k$ Nearest Neighbors Classifier via Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12903)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Shuyin Xia,Hongxuan He,Lifeng Shen,Guoyin Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粒球计算的自适应高效KNN方法（Adaptive-GBKNN），通过粗粒度划分和Fisher准则控制粒球分裂，在预测阶段利用加权距离定位最近粒球并动态确定k值，在多个数据集上均优于现有KNN变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KNN算法被广泛使用，但k值的选择对性能影响显著，是亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段：训练阶段先对数据集进行粗粒度划分以降低粒球内数据分布复杂度，引入Fisher准则控制粒球的分裂与停止，生成多粒度粒球表示；预测阶段通过加权距离机制定位最近的粒球，并在测试样本周围构建自适应邻域，该邻域内实际包含的样本数即动态确定的有效k值。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在多个数据集上的准确率和效率均优于现有KNN变体。

Conclusion: 基于粒球计算的KNN方法通过最近粒球诱导的邻域提供了更稳定的局部组信息，增强了对噪声和局部扰动的鲁棒性，同时实现了自适应的k值选择与高效的预测。代码已开源以供复现。

Abstract: The $k$-Nearest Neighbor~(KNN) algorithm is widely used across various tasks. The selection of the $k$ value is a key issue because it significantly impacts performance. In this paper, an adaptive and efficient KNN approach via granular-ball computing is proposed. The method consists of two stages. \textcolor{black}{In the training stage, the dataset is first coarsely partitioned to reduce the complexity of data distributions within a granular ball, and then the Fisher criterion is introduced to control ball splitting and stopping, yielding a multi-granularity granular ball representation. In the prediction stage, the nearest granular ball is first located through a weighted distance mechanism, and an adaptive neighborhood is then constructed around the test sample. The effective $k$ value is dynamically determined by the actual number of samples contained in this neighborhood. The neighborhood induced by the nearest granular ball provides more stable local group information, thereby improving robustness against noise and local perturbations.} Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing KNN variants across multiple datasets in terms of both accuracy and efficiency. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/Adaptive-GBKNN.

</details>


### [294] [EGRL: Edge generation-guided relation-aware learning for RNA-protein interaction prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12906)
*Danyu Li,Ling Zhou,Rubing Huang,Xian Zhong,Bin Zou,Kui Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EGRL框架，用于RNA-蛋白质相互作用预测。通过隐式元路径学习、多关系感知注意力机制、图生成器和多特征融合预测器，解决了现有GNN方法在数据稀疏性和冷启动场景下泛化能力不足的问题，在四个基准数据集上取得竞争性表现，并在未知分子上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNA-蛋白质相互作用（RPI）对细胞功能调控至关重要，传统湿实验成本高、耗时。虽然深度学习方法（特别是GNN）为RPI预测提供了高效替代方案，但现有基于GNN的方法依赖同构图或预定义元路径，限制了其处理数据稀疏性的能力，且难以泛化到涉及未知分子的冷启动场景。

Method: EGRL框架包含四个关键组件：(1) 隐式元路径学习，无需手工设计路径即可捕获关系语义；(2) 多关系感知注意力机制，实现交互模式的适应性融合；(3) 图生成器，预测潜在"软边"以支持冷启动节点；(4) 多特征融合预测器用于最终交互评分。模型通过主任务损失和辅助生成器损失进行联合训练。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的全面评估表明，EGRL取得了具有竞争力的整体性能。在冷启动设置下表现尤为突出，对未知分子达到AUROC 0.867和AUPR 0.861，相比先前最先进方法在AUROC上提升8.6%，在AUPR上提升5.0%。

Conclusion: EGRL通过隐式元路径学习和软边生成机制，有效缓解了数据稀疏性问题，并显著提升了冷启动场景下对未知分子的泛化能力，为RPI预测提供了一种更鲁棒且通用的深度学习框架。

Abstract: RNA-Protein Interactions (RPIs) are critical for regulating cellular functions. While traditional wet-lab experiments for RPI detection are costly and time-consuming, Deep Learning (DL) methods provide an efficient computational alternative for RPI Prediction (RPIP). In particular, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are promising, as they naturally model RPI networks. However, existing GNN-based methods often rely on homogeneous graphs or predefined meta-paths, which limit their ability to handle data sparsity and to generalize to cold-start scenarios involving unknown molecules. To address these limitations, we propose Edge Generation-guided Relation-aware Learning (EGRL), a novel framework with several key components: implicit meta-path learning to capture relational semantics without handcrafted paths; a multi-relation-aware attention mechanism for adaptive fusion of interaction patterns; a graph generator that predicts potential ("soft") edges to support cold-start nodes; and a multi-feature fusion predictor for final interaction scoring. EGRL is jointly trained with a primary task loss and an auxiliary generator loss. Comprehensive evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EGRL achieves competitive overall performance. More importantly, it exhibits superior generalization in cold-start settings, achieving an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.867 and an Area Under the Precision-Recall curve (AUPR) of 0.861 on unknown molecules, corresponding to improvements of 8.6% in AUROC and 5.0% in AUPR over prior state-of-the-art methods. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [295] [Revisiting Overestimation Bias Problem of Q-learning: Settling Large Discrete Action Space via Action Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12912)
*Pu Li,Tao Tan,Hong Xie,Xiaoyu Shi,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模动作空间下Q-learning的过估计偏差问题，提出"动作交集"（action intersection）策略，通过让两个Q函数共享部分轨迹数据（共享时用耦合范式更新、不共享时用解耦范式更新），实现半解耦，从而在大范围偏差（从欠估计到过估计）和精细粒度上灵活控制估计偏差，在深度强化学习中大幅超越多个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模动作空间中，Q值估计的随机性增大，导致现有两大范式（耦合范式和解耦范式）各自存在瓶颈：耦合范式因随机性使某些动作被高估而产生正偏差，解耦范式因两个独立Q表对同一动作的估计间隙增大而产生负偏差。作者希望找到一种简单而有效的策略来缓解这两大瓶颈。

Method: 提出"动作交集"（action intersection）策略，实现半解耦：两个Q函数共享一定比例的轨迹数据；若数据样本被共享，则用耦合范式更新（最优动作与Q值用同一Q函数估计）；否则用解耦范式更新（用两个独立Q函数）。通过调节数据共享比例（即动作交集大小），可以在欠估计到过估计之间连续调节偏差，且交集大小可任意细化以精确控制。

Result: 在深度强化学习实验中，该方法大幅优于多个SOTA基线；在表格（tabular）实验中揭示了方法取得优越性能的内在原因。

Conclusion: 动作交集策略是一种简单而强大的方法，兼具大偏差调节范围和精细化控制能力，能有效缓解大规模动作空间下Q-learning的过估计偏差问题。

Abstract: This paper considers the overestimation bias problem of Q-learning in the setting of a large action space, for the purpose of relieving the bottleneck of existing methods. We find that the large action space increases the randomness in Q-value estimation. The randomness makes two paradigms that drive the major literature on the overestimation problem have their own bottlenecks: the coupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with the same Q-function, always has a positive bias. This is because randomness leads to some actions having abnormally high estimated values than their true values, and the coupling methods prefer these actions. The decoupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with two independent Q-functions, always has a negative bias. This is because randomness increases the estimation gap between the two independent Q-tables for the same action. This paper shows that action intersection can be a simple yet powerful strategy to relieve these bottlenecks. The action intersection strategy enables semi-decoupling via two designs: (1) it allows two Q-functions to share a certain fraction of trajectory data; (2) if a data sample is shared, each Q-function is updated using the coupling paradigm; otherwise, using the decoupling paradigm. Two properties make the action intersection strategy powerful: (1) attaining a large bias range, i.e., varying the data sharing fraction, the estimation bias varies from underestimating to overestimating; (2) fine granularity: the action intersection size can be made arbitrarily finer to enable finer control. We consider two experiment settings, i.e., tabular and deep RL, deep RL experiments show that our method outperforms several SOTA baselines drastically; tabular experiments reveal why our method can achieve superior performance.

</details>


### [296] [Towards Socially Compliant Navigation in Deep Reinforcement Learning via Proxemics-Based Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12917)
*Takieddine Soualhi,Jacques Saraydaryan,Laetitia Matignon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于proxemics（人际距离学）理论的密集、可解释的社会奖励函数，用于DRL社交导航，在保持导航效率的同时提升社会合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习（DRL）导航方法虽然提升了拥挤环境下的导航性能，但过度关注任务导向目标，对社会合规目标的表征不足，导致机器人在人群中缺乏符合社交规范的行为。

Method: 基于Hall的proxemics理论，将每个人的个人空间建模为径向高斯混合场，并在机器人视野内计算以机器人为中心的局部代价，形成密集且可解释的社会学习信号作为奖励函数，集成到现有的DRL导航方法中。

Result: 在多种人群场景、奖励基线和人群密度下的仿真实验中，所提出的奖励函数在保持与对比奖励模型相当的导航性能的同时，持续提升了社会指标。

Conclusion: 该方法为DRL社交导航提供了一种兼顾社会合规与导航效率的有效奖励设计范式，验证了proxemics理论在机器人导航奖励建模中的实用价值。

Abstract: Developing effective robot navigation methods in crowded environments is essential for real-world applications. Although recent deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods have improved navigation performance in crowded environments, they often focus primarily on task-centric objectives and underrepresent social compliance objectives. In this paper, we introduce a novel proxemics-based reward formulation for DRL social navigation that provides a dense, interpretable social learning signal while maintaining navigation efficiency. Our approach models each human's personal space as a radial Gaussian-mixture field derived from Hall's proxemics theory and computes a robot-centric local cost over the robot's field of view. We integrate the proposed reward into established DRL navigation methods and evaluate it in simulation across multiple crowd scenarios, reward baselines, and crowd densities using both navigation metrics and social metrics. Results show that the proposed reward consistently improves social metrics in simulation while maintaining competitive navigation performance relative to the compared reward models.

</details>


### [297] [Momentum as Residual-Driven Multiplier Correction for Deep Learning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12925)
*Zhixin Ren,Yau Lyu,Congrong Li,Liping Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM框架，基于残差惩罚变量分裂将动量解释为类ADMM乘子修正，并由此设计RADAR优化器，融合相对论自适应几何、解耦残差修正与二阶动量过滤，在视觉、语言和强化学习任务上超越现有自适应优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于动量的优化器在深度学习中广泛使用，但动量递归、更新几何与加速之间的内在关系尚未被充分理解，缺乏统一的理论框架来解析这些机制。

Method: 提出AIM框架，基于残差惩罚变量分裂（类似ADMM）将动量解释为乘子式修正项；分离两个通常交织的机制：残差惩罚决定更新几何，目标相关子问题的近似决定加速形式。在此基础上提出RADAR优化器，结合相对论自适应几何、解耦残差修正和二阶动量过滤；并通过方差扰动的Lyapunov漂移分析建立随机收敛性。

Result: 在监督视觉学习、语言建模和强化学习等多个任务上，RADAR相比强自适应优化器基线取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: AIM框架为理解动量优化器提供了新的统一视角，RADAR作为具体实例验证了该框架的有效性，展示了将动量递归与更新几何解耦设计的实用价值。

Abstract: Momentum-based optimizers are widely used in modern deep learning, yet the relations among momentum recursion, update geometry, and acceleration remain only partially understood. We develop an $\textbf{A}$DMM-$\textbf{I}$nspired $\textbf{M}$omentum (AIM) framework based on residual-penalty variable splitting, which interprets momentum as a multiplier-like correction driven by the splitting residual. AIM recovers the exponential moving average of gradients from an ADMM-style multiplier update and separates two mechanisms that are usually intertwined in practical optimizers: the residual penalty determines the update geometry, whereas the approximation of the objective-related subproblem determines the acceleration form. Building on AIM, we propose $\textbf{R}$elativistic $\textbf{A}$daptive gradient $\textbf{D}$escent with $\textbf{A}$ccelerated $\textbf{R}$esidual (RADAR), which combines relativistic adaptive geometry, decoupled residual correction, and second-order momentum filtering to improve the update direction and momentum estimation. We establish stochastic convergence through a variance-perturbed Lyapunov drift analysis. Experiments on supervised vision learning, language modeling, and reinforcement learning show that RADAR achieves consistent improvements over strong adaptive optimizer baselines.

</details>


### [298] [H-VAEP and H-xT: Valuing Offensive On-the-Ball Actions in Handball by Estimating Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12926)
*Julius Broermann,Oliver Müller,Michael Döring,Jochen Baumeister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将足球领域的Expected Threat (xT)和VAEP两种事件级动作估值框架系统性地适配到手球运动，利用德甲手球联赛（Handball Bundesliga）五个赛季的追踪数据开发了Handball-xT（H-xT）和Handball-VAEP（H-VAEP）模型，并通过模拟和实验验证了其稳健性与球员评分的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手球球员评估依赖基础技术统计或启发式指数，无法体现多球员参与的进攻配合链贡献。虽然足球分析领域已广泛采用xT和VAEP等事件级动作估值框架，但这些方法尚未被适配到手球运动，存在方法论空白。

Method: 本文利用Handball Bundesliga五个赛季的追踪派生事件数据，分别对xT和VAEP进行手球化改造：H-xT采用手球专用的场地分区布局（非标准矩形网格），并通过仿真证明其系统性更强的稳健性；H-VAEP则通过定制特征空间和选择适当的上下文长度来优化模型，以减少球队身份泄漏（team-identity leakage）。最终开源完整代码库。

Result: 实验表明H-xT在手球场景下比标准矩形网格分区更稳健；H-VAEP产生了极其稳定、具有区分度和直觉解释力的球员评分，能够突出进攻组织（build-up）阶段的贡献。

Conclusion: 本文填充了手球运动缺乏现代事件级动作估值工具的研究空白，验证了xT和VAEP框架的跨运动可迁移性，并通过开源代码促进职业俱乐部实际部署应用。

Abstract: Traditional player evaluation in professional handball relies on basic box-score metrics or heuristic indices, which fail to credit the multi-player build-up chain. While football (soccer) analytics has adopted Expected Threat (xT) and Valuing Actions by Estimating Probabilities (VAEP), these event-based action valuation frameworks have not yet been adapted to handball. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive adaptation and evaluation of xT and VAEP for handball, utilizing five seasons of tracking-derived event data from the Handball Bundesliga. We develop Handball-xT (H-xT) using a handball-native court zoning layout, demonstrating via simulations that it is systematically more robust than standard rectangular grids. We optimize Handball-VAEP (H-VAEP) by tailoring its feature space and selecting the context length to limit team-identity leakage. Our evaluation shows that H-VAEP yields exceptionally stable, discriminative, and intuitive player ratings that highlight build-up play. Finally, we release our complete code repository to help professional clubs deploy these models.

</details>


### [299] [Multi-perspective Imbalance-Conscious 6G Beamforming Optimization and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12929)
*Chukwunonso Henry Nwokoye,Blessing Oluchi Iloka,Chikwue V. Umeugoji,Christopher Anene Egemba,Nnenna D. Duroha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了6G-IoT波束赋形优化（6GBO）的机器学习方法，对比了网络、环境、设备和视觉四类特征组的预测能力，并结合无监督聚类与可解释性分析揭示了关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G-IoT波束赋形优化需要有效的机器学习方法，但缺乏对不同特征组预测能力及无监督聚类的系统性比较研究。

Method: 采用有监督学习（分类/预测模型）与无监督学习（K-means、DBSCAN、层次聚类）相结合的方法，通过不平衡感知实验评估四类特征组，并使用Elbow法、轮廓系数和Davies-Bouldin指数评估聚类质量，辅以全局特征重要性分析进行可解释性研究。

Result: 网络特征组的预测性能（召回率、F1分数、ROC-AUC）优于设备、环境和视觉特征组；无监督聚类主要受部署环境和设备类型影响而非移动性属性；带宽、IoT传感器和移动性具有较高的全局特征重要性。

Conclusion: 网络特征对6GBO预测最为关键，后续将引入深度学习和强化学习以预测吞吐量/时延或基于SNR增强等性能指标优化奖励。

Abstract: The study presents a systematic machine learning (ML) study of 6G-IoT beamforming optimization (6GBO) using supervised and unsupervised approaches. We compared the predictive power of network, environmental, device, and vision feature groups for 6GBO. Additionally, it addressed other unsupervised perspectives that can enhance 6GBO, including clustering network scenarios using methods such as K-means, DBSCAN, and hierarchical clustering. Several imbalance-aware experiments revealed that network features possess better prediction power than device, environmental, and vision feature groups, as evidenced by their recall, F1-score and ROC-AUC values. For unsupervised ML exploration (assessed using Elbow, Silhouette score, and Davies-Bouldin Index methods), the results indicate that the deployment environment and type of device primarily influence clustering, rather than mobility-based attributes. Furthermore, the explainability analysis showed that bandwidth, IoT sensors, and mobility possess higher global feature importance across the feature groups. In the future, we would apply deep and reinforcement learning techniques to predict throughput/latency or to optimize rewards determined by performance indicators like SNR enhancement

</details>


### [300] [Diagnosing JEPA World Models with Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12939)
*Guo An,Zijing Wu,Honghua Dong,Yuhao Yan,Zixuan Gui,Haochong Chen,Shanzhao Ruan,Xiang Wang,Yurong Ling,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动作条件预测一致性（ACPC）诊断指标及其衍生度量（不变半径IR与分离率SR），用于量化联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）类世界模型在视觉扰动下的鲁棒性，并证明该度量可上界扰动引起的预测误差与规划代价变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类架构虽通过在紧致隐空间预测来规避像素级建模压力，但缺乏对视觉扰动的鲁棒性保证——扰动仍可能改变编码表征进而影响后续动作条件预测。双模拟（bisimulation）准则精确刻画了这一需求：仅当两个观测的动作条件后果一致时才应视为同一状态。基于此准则，需要一个可操作的诊断工具来量化模型对视觉扰动的敏感程度。

Method: 提出ACPC成对诊断度量，衡量干净历史序列与视觉扰动视图在同一动作序列下滚动前向后的发散程度。理论上证明该发散度上界了扰动引起的多步预测误差变化与规划器代价变化。基于成对ACPC进一步定义两个互补度量：不变半径（IR）汇总干净-扰动滚动轨迹的散布范围，分离率（SR）检验不同状态在滚动后是否仍可区分。

Result: 在四个视觉控制任务上的实验表明：成对ACPC能有效预测扰动引起的预测误差与代价变化；在LeWM上IR-SR筛查可跨任务迁移，联合诊断在模糊与缩放扰动下仍具信息量；PLDM在不同架构下呈现类似的诊断趋势。

Conclusion: ACPC及其衍生度量IR-SR为JEPA类世界模型提供了可验证、可迁移的视觉鲁棒性诊断框架，既能量化扰动的有害影响，又能同时确保不同状态的可区分性，为世界模型的鲁棒性评估与设计提供了理论支撑与实践工具。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models that predict in a compact latent space rather than in pixels, reducing the pressure to model nuisance appearance. Yet this provides no guarantee against visual perturbations: they can still alter the encoded representation and affect subsequent action-conditioned predictions. Bisimulation captures this requirement precisely: two observations should be treated as the same state only when their action-conditioned consequences agree. Guided by this criterion, we introduce Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency (ACPC), a diagnostic that measures how far a clean history and a visually perturbed view of it diverge after being rolled forward under the same action sequence. We prove that this divergence bounds the perturbation-induced change in multi-step prediction error and planner cost. Building on pairwise ACPC, we define two complementary measures: the Invariance Radius (IR) summarizes clean-perturbed rollout spread, while the Separation Rate (SR) checks whether different states remain distinguishable after rollout. Experiments on four visual control tasks show that pairwise ACPC predicts perturbation-induced prediction and cost changes. On LeWM, the IR-SR screen transfers across tasks, and the joint diagnostic remains informative under blur and resize. PLDM exhibits similar diagnostic trends under a different architecture.

</details>


### [301] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
*Hamza Shafiq,Hung Manh Pham,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CardioState-JEPA，一个跨ECG、PPG和PCG三种心脏传感模态的心脏基础模型，基于生理学感知的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），将异质波形映射到统一token空间，通过预测掩蔽的潜在心脏状态学习共享表征，并利用学习到的延迟对齐器处理跨模态时间偏移。在25个下游任务上，该模型相比最优自监督基线在PPG、PCG和ECG上分别提升了8.2、18.8和15.5个AUROC点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心脏基础模型均针对单一传感模态训练，未能利用ECG、PPG和PCG对同一心脏周期提供的互补视图，即三种模态共享的生理学信息被忽视，导致跨模态的联合学习潜力未被开发。

Method: 采用生理学感知的联合嵌入预测架构：首先将异质波形映射到共同token空间，使用单个共享Transformer编码器处理；预训练目标为预测掩蔽的潜在心脏状态（而非传感器特定的波形外观），从而将学习重点放在共享生理学上；提出学习得到的延迟对齐器（delay aligner）来匹配电学、力学和血液动力学事件之间的时间偏移；训练策略分两阶段——先利用大量单模态数据学习模态内结构，再用配对数据在潜在心脏时间上对齐模态。

Result: 在25个覆盖ECG、PPG、PCG的下游任务上作为冻结编码器进行评估，相比最优自监督信号基线，PPG分类平均提升8.2 AUROC点，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC点，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC点；在多个ECG基准上匹配或超过使用特权的临床文本或有监督标签训练的心脏模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明异质心脏信号可以相互监督并共同支撑一个统一的心脏生理学基础模型，为跨模态心脏基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG), photoplethysmography (PPG), and phonocardiography (PCG) provide complementary views of the same cardiac cycle, yet existing cardiac foundation models are trained for a single sensing modality, leaving the shared physiology across sensors unexploited. We introduce CardioState-JEPA, a cardiac foundation model to learn a single shared representation jointly across ECG, PPG, and PCG, built on a physiology-aware joint-embedding predictive architecture. The model maps heterogeneous waveforms into a common token space, processes them with a single shared Transformer encoder, and learns by predicting masked latent cardiac states, placing the pretraining target on shared physiology rather than sensor-specific waveform appearance. To handle the temporal offsets between electrical, mechanical, and hemodynamic events, cross-modal prediction uses a learned delay aligner that matches signals at the corresponding cardiac time. Because synchronized multi-sensor recordings are scarce, CardioState-JEPA first learns within-modality structure from abundant unimodal data and then uses paired data to align modalities in latent cardiac time. Evaluated as a frozen encoder across 25 downstream tasks spanning ECG, PPG, and PCG, our encoder improves average PPG classification by 8.2 AUROC points, PCG murmur detection by 18.8 AUROC points, and ECG classification by 15.5 AUROC points over the best self-supervised signal baseline and matches or exceeds cardiac models trained with privileged clinical text or supervised labels on several ECG benchmarks. These results establish that heterogeneous cardiac signals can mutually supervise a single foundation model of cardiac physiology.

</details>


### [302] [I-SDPO: Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12957)
