Method: 提出了基于一类支持向量机（OCSVM）的概念漂移检测新方法，与MK-Means和MMD方法对比。实验涵盖四种学习模型（MLP、RF、SVM、XGBoost），在静态场景、周期重训场景和漂移感知场景下评估，并使用Pareto Front分析精度与训练效率之间的权衡。

Result: 三种概念漂移检测技术均能达到与周期重训相当的分类精度，同时训练效率显著更高（需要重训练的模型数量大幅减少）。基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训方法在整体上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。

Conclusion: 研究为恶意软件分类模型中的概念漂移精确检测提供了有力证据，证明基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训策略能在保持分类精度的同时显著提升训练效率，是周期重训的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [336] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑复合目标分布的Moreau-Yosida未校正Langevin算法（MYULA），提出用"参考激活迹"B_ref来刻画离散化误差，替代传统的全局曲率界d/λ，并证明在结构化的分段线性/套索/分组/全变差惩罚下可达到Õ(ε^{-2})精度而非通用的Õ(ε^{-3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MYULA算法求解非光滑复合目标分布时，经典理论依赖全局Lipschitz曲率界d/λ来控制Moreau包络带来的离散化误差，该误差在λ较小时过大，导致理论收敛率仅为Õ(ε^{-3})。实际中Many凸惩罚函数的Moreau包络的Hessian迹远小于全局上界，需要更精细的度量来刻画真实离散化误差。

Method: 引入参考激活迹B_ref（μ_λ起始的MYULA一步热子步中a_λ=tr H_λ的平均），用它替代全局曲率界d/λ控制离散化误差。推导MYULA的改进复杂度N≲(1/m)[L_f+(τ_f+G²+B_ref)/ε²+M_λ/ε]，并给出Moreau偏差界√m W₂(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4。随后对分段线性、套索、分组、全变差等结构化惩罚，通过曲率-管估计证明B_ref与λ无关。

Result: 通用界B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε^{-3})精度依赖；对结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使B_ref独立于λ，从而对相同经典MYULA核获得Õ(ε^{-2})的精度依赖。选择合适的λ≍ε/G²可获得针对π的端到端保证。

Conclusion: 本文通过更精细的参考激活迹替代全局曲率界，揭示了MYULA实际离散化误差仅依赖于沿轨迹的局部Hessian迹而非全局上界，从而在结构化稀疏/分段线性问题上将理论复杂度从Õ(ε^{-3})提升至Õ(ε^{-2})，且无需修改算法核。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [337] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出合成人格预训练（SPP），从预训练的token零开始就将期望的助手人格内置于模型中，通过在预训练文档上添加价值对齐的第一人称反思注释，再通过标准交叉熵损失同时训练原文与反思，最后通过"人格绑定"将期望人格与助手身份绑定。实验表明SPP在3B/500B规模的模型上能改善宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低OOD道德困境中的错位率，同时保持能力，且早期干预的效果优于后期引入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐方法通常在预训练之后才引入，这使价值观成为一种"薄覆盖层"而非深度根基，容易导致后续错位。作者认为价值观应该在预训练阶段就塑造，而非在行为先验形成后再叠加。

Method: SPP包含三步：(1) 用规范价值宪法对预训练文档进行注解，生成价值对齐的第一人称反思；(2) 在标准预训练文档及其反思上使用标准交叉熵损失进行预训练，在多重人格中植入期望人格；(3) 在用户-助手对话数据上进行后期训练，将期望人格绑定到助手身份上（人格绑定）。

Result: 在高达3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP显著改善宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低了OOD道德困境中的错位率，同时保持了下游任务能力。与仅在预训练结束时引入SPP相比，从token零开始进行对齐产生更强的宪法遵循、更明显的价值优先级的转变以及更一致的困境选择；该优势在不进行人格绑定时消失，并随预训练预算增加而增强。

Conclusion: 早期塑造价值观对于对齐至关重要，预训练期的人格干预是一种有效的对齐方法，克服了后期对齐作为"薄覆盖层"的根本局限。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [338] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏正交回归技术（SORT），一种用于从噪声和不规则采样数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT通过L1正则化回归直接从观测中估计展开系数，无需显式求积或解析内积。其核心应用是基于数据驱动的常微分方程发现，通过恢复紧凑的谱表示来辅助系统辨识，相比符号回归和SINDy等方法提供了一条互补路径。实验表明SORT在基函数适配良好时与现有稀疏回归基线相当或更优，且在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配下表现更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统辨识方法（如SINDy、符号回归）依赖有限函数库的脆性选择，当库中缺少问题特定的非线性项时模型可能完全失效。作者希望将问题从"在通用术语中做脆弱选择"转变为"问题域适配的基设计"，从而提供更稳健且可复用的中间谱表示。

Method: SORT的核心方法是将向量场表示在所选正交基中，通过L1正则化回归直接从观测数据估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算。该方法同时支持稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配场景下的系数学习，学习到的谱表示可进一步用于符号形式搜索、非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Result: 在动力学系统实验中，SORT在基适配良好时与库基稀疏回归基线（如SINDy）匹配或更优；在稀疏采样、噪声导数估计和表示失配下退化更稳定。实验还表明主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加而保持，支持阶数一致的模型增长。此外，学习到的展开可支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。

Conclusion: SORT提供了系统辨识、逼近和积分的可复用中间谱表示，将基设计显式纳入科学建模问题中。虽然不能完全免疫表示失配，但它将问题从脆弱的术语选择转向适配问题域的基设计，为数据驱动的方程发现提供了新的互补路径。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [339] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Intern-S2-Preview系列科学智能体基础模型，通过科学多模态预训练和统一的后训练流水线（包括监督微调、多任务强化学习、黑盒/白盒智能体RL和在线蒸馏），构建了支持多模态科学理解、推理、生成和长时程任务的模型。397B参数的旗舰模型在时间序列建模上扩展了能力，并探索了Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径用于快速科学领域特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要AI系统能够在异构模态的科学证据上进行推理、与科学工具和环境交互，并在长任务时间跨度上持续进步。现有模型难以同时满足这些需求，需要专门设计的科学智能体基础模型。

Method: 训练pipeline包括：(1) 科学多模态预训练，基于渲染的科学文档、交错图文数据和多领域科学语料；(2) 统一后训练，包含监督微调、可扩展的多任务强化学习、黑盒与白盒智能体强化学习、在线策略蒸馏。技术上采用部分rollout与离策略校正、自适应长度正则化、在线推测解码、鲁棒多任务优化、迹感知经验组装等技巧。架构上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B扩展了时间序列建模到数值预测，Memory Decoder作为独立记忆增强路径支持快速科学特化而不修改冻结的397B主干。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准测试中，Intern-S2-Preview-397B在多个场景达到竞争力或领先的结果。时间序列模块提升了SciTS上的科学信号理解和预测性能；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升至60.32，且无需修改冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview系列展示了面向科学发现的智能体基础模型的完整训练范式，验证了多模态预训练+统一后训练pipeline的有效性，同时探索了时间序列建模和记忆增强路径作为科学领域能力补充的可行性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [340] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，将患者表示为结构化潜在状态，通过时间排序的术后事件逐步演化，用于预测房颤消融后的长期复发风险。在DECAAF-II上达到AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777，以及疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到未来终点的单步映射，忽略了恢复过程的时序性和不规则性。实际临床中，术后观察、用药调整、重复干预和生理测量都是异步记录的，且会随时间改变风险评估，因此需要能够建模这种动态演化过程的模型。

Method: 该方法包括：（1）将基线影像编码为3D空间潜在状态；（2）利用手术情境信息、静态协变量、经过时间和围事件生理嵌入来更新该潜在状态；（3）随访影像仅提供训练阶段的潜在预测监督信号。模型被应用于房颤消融场景，利用90天恢复窗口内的不规则术后记录来预测长期复发风险。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上的重复内部交叉验证中，模型的复发预测达到了AUROC 0.756和AUPRC 0.777；疤痕范围预测的MAE为2.971个百分点，且在推理时无需随访MRI强度数据。学习到的潜在状态还支持不同时间范围的风险查询和对空窗期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 该干预感知的世界模型能够有效建模术后不规则的临床事件轨迹，利用90天窗口内的问诊和生理记录提升长期复发预测能力，且无需推理阶段的随访影像，同时学习到的潜在表示具有灵活的风险查询和输入编辑能力。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [341] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是首个在仓储级别评估联合实现与证明合成的基准，包含43个来自真实项目的多模块Lean 4实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq等语言及多个领域。最强智能体仅完全解决27/43个实例，在最难仓储上无规范可闭合，表明当前智能体在仓储规模验证软件合成上仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编程智能体生成的代码缺乏正确性保证，虽然已验证代码生成（同时产生实现与机器检查证明）提供了更可靠的路径，但现有基准仅聚焦单个函数或仅评估给定实现下的证明生成，无法回答智能体能否在真实多模块代码库中做出连贯的实现与证明决策这一开放问题。

Method: Vero基准包含43个来自真实仓库的多模块实例，覆盖Python、Dafny、Verus、Coq等多种语言及密码协议、分布式系统等领域。每个实例是一个包含预定API接口、人工整理的正式规范与参考实现的Lean 4多模块仓库，支持仅证明和代码加证明两种评估模式。Vero还设计了审计机制，允许智能体正式证明规范不可满足或参考代码不正确，以暴露和修正策展过程中的潜在错误。

Result: 评估了配备Lean工具链的前沿编码智能体配置，最强智能体仅完全解决43个实例中的27个，且在难度最高的仓库上没有任何规范能被闭合，表明当前智能体在仓储规模验证软件合成任务上仍远未达到可靠水平。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓储规模已验证软件合成的进展提供了具体测试平台，揭示了当前智能体在该任务上的显著不足。工作已发布基准、策展流程和评估框架以供社区使用，推动该方向进一步发展。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [342] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的自回归校正头从链扩展到树结构，通过在单批次中扩展和评分所有节点构建固定宽度候选树，并使用最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，在数学、代码和聊天基准测试中实现了最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高每次验证接受12.97个token，相对DFlash提升98.6%，相对Domino提升27.9%，达到9.73倍的损失无关加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token来无损加速自回归语言模型，但现有方法存在不足：基于扩散的草稿生成器预测整块token，但其位置分布是边缘分布而非沿草稿路径的条件分布；现有的递归校正方法仅沿单条草稿链引入因果信息，而扩散树构建虽然扩大候选覆盖范围但没有在单独分支上携带校正信息。因此需要一种方法既能利用树结构扩大候选覆盖率，又能沿各分支进行递归校正。

Method: DARTree首先通过单批次扩展和评分候选树每深度的所有节点来构建固定宽度候选树，然后仅应用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将预训练AR校正头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦。该方法将AR校正头从链式结构扩展到树结构，实现训练无关的投机解码。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准测试上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置中均取得最高平均接受长度和加速比，每次验证轮次最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，并达到最高9.73倍的无损加速比（相对于本地测量的自回归解码）。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将AR校正头从链扩展到树，成功结合了树构建的广泛候选覆盖与递归校正的因果信息优势，在多种基准和配置下均取得最优性能，证明了树结构递归校正在投机解码中的有效性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [343] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类与二值分割中逐实例Jaccard分数（IoU）的校准理论。作者证明了Jaccard得分矩阵、移位损失矩阵和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性及仿射维度性质，给出了精确校准的CCdim上下界，并指出零回归校准需要指数级预测坐标，而任意固定可加回归容忍度则可用多项式维度实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard分数（IoU）是多标签分类和分割中的标准度量，但其校准理论（calibration）尚未被系统研究。作者希望理解Jaccard损失的精确校准需要多少预测坐标，以及是否存在高效的近似校准方案，从而为设计具有可证明回归保证的替代损失（surrogate loss）提供理论依据。

Method: 作者采用多种技术手段：(1) 结合有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演来证明Jaccard相关损失矩阵的非奇异性和仿射维度；(2) 通过因子加权分布（factorial weighted distribution）构造$2^{s-1}+1$个支持结果来证明校准维度（CCdim）的下界；(3) 提出了一种新的$F_1$-到-Jaccard转换，将已有的$(s^2+1)$维$F_1$替代损失转化为多项式时间的Jaccard规则；(4) 设计MinHash平方损失替代函数及其有符号变体，给出具体的维度界和概率保证。

Result: 证明了Jaccard损失在$\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$约定下，其损失矩阵非奇异，损失列仿射维度为$2^s-1$。精确校准的CCdim满足$2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$。$F_1$-到-Jaccard转移给出渐近Jaccard回归至多$3-2\sqrt{2}$。MinHash平方损失替代函数对任意$\alpha>0$和$0<\rho<1$，直接构造维度为$O((s^2+s\log(1/\rho))/\alpha^2)$，有符号变体维度为$O((s+\log(1/\rho))/\alpha^2)$，以至少$1-\rho$的概率达到Jaccard回归floor $\alpha$。

Conclusion: 零回归精确校准要求指数级预测维度，而任意固定的可加回归容忍度都可以用多项式预测维度实现。这一结果为Jaccard/IoU度量的替代损失设计提供了明确的理论边界，表明在实际应用中选择近似校准方案（如MinHash平方损失）比追求精确校准更具可行性。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [344] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Defensive Booster算法，一个简单的防御性预测算法，同时获得在线梯度提升的Brier分数竞争性保证和在线弱到强提升的弱学习条件下的分类误差保证，且只需访问单个弱分类器，比现有方法效率高几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术分别提供两种互不兼容的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的跨度诱导的最佳预测器竞争，但当跨度中不含准确预测器时无任何保证；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差驱动到零，但当该条件不满足时几乎无保证。作者希望同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 作者提出Defensive Booster算法，通过操作化提升的"二元视角"（dual view）：当算法随机分类误差持续偏高时，其错误权重构成一个平滑的重加权，该重加权上所有弱假设的边（edge）都很低，从而产生事后硬核（ex-post hard-core）证书证明弱学习条件不成立。算法只需访问一个弱类学习器，并开发了强自适应变体，在任意时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: 实验表明Defensive Booster在合成和真实数据流上表现强劲，有时大幅超越所有先前基线，同时运行速度快几个数量级。

Conclusion: 作者通过单一弱学习器同时实现了Brier分数竞争性保证和弱学习条件下的误差收敛保证，解决了在线提升中两种保证不可兼得的难题，且效率显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [345] [New optimal linear codes over $\ZZ_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12414)
*Hopein Christofen Tang,Djoko Suprijanto*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了在ZZ_4上从已知线性码构造新线性码的新方法，成功获得了许多新码，其中大量是最优码，特别是找到了k1=2,k2=0时的所有最优码以及k1=3,k2=0时的许多最优码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在环ZZ_4上构造线性码是编码理论中的一个重要研究方向，之前已有的构造方法有限，需要开发新方法来获得更多（尤其是最优的）线性码。

Method: 提出了从已知ZZ_4线性码构造新线性码的新方法，通过某种变换或组合手段在环上生成新的码字集合，系统地搜索并获得最优码。

Result: 成功构造出许多新的线性码，其中大量是最优码；完整确定了参数为k1=2,k2=0的所有最优码，并为k1=3,k2=0的情况找到了许多最优码。

Conclusion: 所提出的构造方法有效，能够系统地生成ZZ_4上新的最优线性码，填补了该参数范围内最优码的空白。

Abstract: In this work, we present novel approaches for constructing linear codes over $\ZZ_4$ from the known ones. We succeeded in obtaining new linear codes, many of which are optimal. In particular, we found all optimal codes for $k_1=2,~k_2=0$ and many optimal codes for $k_1=3,~k_2=0.$

</details>


### [346] [Channel Estimation for OTFS Systems With Overspread Doppler Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12524)
*Preety Priya,Yi Hong,Emanuele Viterbo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对非地面多卫星链路中具有超扩散多普勒频移的OTFS系统，提出了一种DD域训练帧结构和两阶段信道估计方法，以解决传统方法在多普勒频移超过子载波间隔一半时因混叠而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非地面多卫星链路等时变信道中，超扩散多普勒频移（幅度大于子载波间隔一半）会导致DD域中因OTFS取模运算产生多普勒混叠，使传统信道估计方法失效，亟需新的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种DD域训练帧和两阶段信道估计方法。训练帧由余弦导频信号和导频符号组成。第一阶段利用DD域导频符号估计传播路径的时延、混叠多普勒频移和信道增益；第二阶段将接收时域信号转换到频域，利用余弦导频信号检测所有多普勒频移的峰值，并通过基于阈值的配对方法将估计的实际多普勒频移与对应的时延和信道增益进行匹配。文章还讨论了所提方法的复杂度。

Result: 在不同场景下，以归一化均方误差（NMSE）和误码率（BER）为指标验证了所提信道估计方法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段信道估计方法能够有效应对超扩散多普勒频移引起的混叠问题，实现对OTFS系统信道的准确估计，并在NMSE和BER方面表现出良好性能，同时具有可接受的复杂度。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) system in time-varying channels with overspread Doppler shifts, typically found in non-terrestrial multi-satellite links. The overspread Doppler shifts with magnitude greater than half of the subcarrier spacing, result in aliased Doppler shifts in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain due to the OTFS modulo operation. This makes channel estimation very challenging and the traditional channel estimation methods become ineffective. To address this challenge, we propose a DD training frame and a two-stage channel estimation method. The training frame comprises a cosine pilot signal and a pilot symbol. In the first stage of the channel estimation, the pilot symbol in the DD domain is utilized to estimate the delays, aliased Doppler shifts, and channel gains of the propagation paths. In the second stage, the received time domain signal is converted into the frequency domain to detect the peaks of all the Doppler shifts using the cosine pilot signal. Then, we present a threshold-based method to pair the estimated actual Doppler shifts with their corresponding delays and channel gains. The complexity of the proposed channel estimation is also discussed. Finally, the performance of the proposed channel estimation is validated in terms of the normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER) in various scenarios.

</details>


### [347] [On Nearly-Perfect Covering Codes Beyond Radius One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12595)
*Gabriel Sac Himelfarb,Moshe Schwartz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究二进制近完美覆盖码（nearly-perfect covering codes）。作者证明对于覆盖半径R≥2，不存在达到原始Van Wee界等号的码，因为原始界未考虑码的最小距离。作者进一步改进了Van Wee界使其包含最小距离参数，并证明了对于R=2,3，所有近完美覆盖码都等价于其构造的码；对于任何R≥3，近完美覆盖码至多有限个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近完美覆盖码是覆盖码理论中的核心研究对象，类似于近完美纠错码在纠错码理论中的地位。R=1的情形已被完全分类，但R≥2的情形此前缺乏系统研究。原始Van Wee界忽略了最小距离的影响，导致无法正确刻画R≥2时的近完美覆盖码，因此需要改进该界并进行分类研究。

Method: 1) 首先证明原始Van Wee界达到等号时，最小距离必须为1或2（对于R≥2时最小距离为1，R=1时可能为1或2），从而排除了R≥2时的可能性；2) 改进了Van Wee不等式，使其显式依赖于码的最小距离d；3) 构造了达到改进后界等号（即近完美）的覆盖码；4) 通过证明此类码的结构性质（如最小距离的约束、码字的分布特征），分类了R=2,3的所有情况；5) 利用结构性质推导出R≥3时近完美覆盖码的有限性。

Result: 证明了R≥2时不存在满足原始Van Wee界等号的码；改进了Van Wee界使其包含最小距离d的影响；构造了R=2,3的（所有）近完美覆盖码并证明了等价性分类；证明了R≥3时近完美覆盖码至多有限个。

Conclusion: 这项工作完整解决了R=2,3情形的近完美覆盖码分类问题，同时为R≥3给出了有限性结论，弥补了原始Van Wee界在R≥2情形的适用性缺陷，是覆盖码理论的重要推进。

Abstract: We study (binary) nearly-perfect covering codes, which are codes that attain the Van Wee bound with equality. They act as the covering counterparts to nearly-perfect error-correcting codes, which attain the Johnson bound with equality. These codes have been completely classified for covering radius $R=1$. We prove that no code with $R\geq 2$ can attain the original Van Wee bound with equality, since it omits the dependence on the minimum distance of the code. We refine the bound to account for the minimum distance and show some nearly-perfect covering codes. By proving some structural properties of such codes, we prove all nearly-perfect covering codes with $R=2,3$ must be equivalent to the codes we showed. We also prove that for any $R\geq 3$, there are at most a finite number of nearly-perfect covering codes.

</details>


### [348] [A Theory of Probabilistic Power Provisioning for Data Centers with Distributed Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12993)
*Can Emre Koksal,Richard A. Barry,Artun Sel*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率框架，用于联合表征配置功率、储能容量和超额取电概率之间的关系，将储能辅助供电分为"小电池区"和"大电池区"两个运行区域，并引入"有效功率"（effective power）概念作为有效带宽的类比，以渐进紧致地刻画大电池区所需的供电余量。通过三个生产数据中心的实测负载验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载的功耗需求不断增长且波动剧烈，使得电力供应成为数据中心运营的关键约束。分布式储能虽然可降低支撑随机负载所需的供电容量，但其收益根本上取决于负载需求的统计特性和时间尺度。当前缺乏一个统一的概率框架来联合刻画功率供给、储能容量与超额取电概率之间的权衡关系。

Method: 1) 建立概率框架，联合表征配置功率、储能容量和超额取电（overdraw）概率；2) 将储能辅助供电划分为小电池区和大电池区两个运行区域并分别分析；3) 在大电池区引入"有效功率"（effective power）概念，作为有效带宽的时间统计类比，给出渐近紧致的表征；4) 量化时间相关性和空间聚合对储能需求及统计复用增益的影响；5) 扩展到多时间尺度需求负载；6) 使用来自HPC、GPU训练和云服务三类生产数据中心的实际功耗轨迹进行验证。

Result: 小电池区中超额取电由短期需求尖峰主导，储能提供近乎线性的供电余量缩减；大电池区中超额取电由更长时间跨度的持续需求波动引起，供电余量的缩减随储能增加呈现边际递减。实测的三种异构、周期平稳且多模态的工作负载均展现出理论预测的运行区域，且一个简单的四参数两状态模型即可捕获其储能-供电权衡的动态特性。

Conclusion: 该框架为下一代AI数据中心提供储能辅助供电的概率理论基础和实用的容量规划原则，涵盖功率配置、储能容量设计以及超额取电风险控制。

Abstract: The growing power demands and variability of AI workloads make electrical power delivery a critical constraint in data-center operation. Distributed energy storage can reduce the power capacity required to support stochastic loads, but its benefits depend fundamentally on the statistics and time scales of demand. This paper develops a probabilistic framework that jointly characterizes provisioned power, energy-storage capacity, and the probability of overdraw. We show that storage-assisted provisioning separates into two operating regimes. In the Small Battery Region, overdraw is dominated by short-lived demand excursions and storage provides nearly linear reductions in the required power margin. In the Large Battery Region, overdraw results from sustained demand fluctuations over longer spans of time, and the required margin exhibits diminishing returns with storage. For this regime we introduce effective power, an analogue of effective bandwidth that captures the temporal statistics of the demand and gives an asymptotically tight characterization of the required power. We further quantify how temporal correlation and spatial aggregation affect storage requirements and statistical multiplexing gains, and extend the analysis to loads with multiple demand time scales. Finally, we evaluate the framework using power-demand traces from three production data centers spanning HPC, GPU-training, and cloud-service workloads. Despite their heterogeneous, cyclo-stationary and multi-modal behavior, the measured workloads exhibit the predicted regimes, and a simple four-parameter two-state model captures the dynamics governing their storage-power tradeoffs. The resulting framework provides both a probabilistic foundation and practical dimensioning principles for storage-assisted power provisioning in next-generation AI data centers.

</details>


### [349] [Deterministic Johnson--Lindenstrauss Projections from Pisot $β$-Transformations for Zero-Knowledge Private Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13078)
*I. Dey,I. Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Pisot β-变换的确定性公共投影矩阵，替代零知识证明中高成本且存在泄漏风险的随机Johnson-Lindenstrauss投影。该投影通过β-映射的谱隙与分析相关性几何衰减（而非等分布）来保证性质，其诱导的平方范数估计量无偏（误差随采样间隔几何衰减），方差为V0/m（V0维度无关）。在六个标准投影（含Yu等人的混沌序列矩阵）对比中，统计质量在测量噪声内相当，并且是唯一同时免于电路内随机性、可在有限域中精确复现、单步成本为log2β而非2^k的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明中，认证消息属于语义类别需要将高维嵌入与类别质心比较，其开销随嵌入维度d增长。随机JL投影虽可将维度降至m≪d，但随机矩阵需要在电路内提交并证明其采样过程，既昂贵又存在信息泄漏风险。因此需要一个公共、确定性且低成本的投影方案。

Method: 从Pisot β-变换的标准轨道构造公共确定性投影，利用β-映射的谱隙和相关性几何衰减进行分析（而非等分布）。证明诱导的平方范数估计量无偏至随采样间隔几何衰减的项，方差为V0/m且V0维度无关（在实验与一个浓度假设下均成立）。通过搜索找到单一公共种子以保持所有成对质心距离，并在固定有限域中逐步骤以log2β成本精确复现。

Result: 在与六种标准投影（包括Yu等人的混沌序列矩阵）的对比中，构造方法在统计质量上与测量噪声范围内相当，且是唯一同时具备免电路内随机性、有限域精确可复现、单步成本log2β（而非2^k）特性的方法。

Conclusion: 该确定性Pisot β-变换投影为零知识证明中的维度压缩提供了一种更安全、更高效且可精确复现的替代方案，消除了随机JL投影中电路内采样带来的成本和泄漏风险。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge (ZK) proofs certify that a message belongs to an allowed semantic class without revealing the message, but the certificate compares a high-dimensional embedding against class centroids, so its cost grows with the embedding dimension $d$. A Johnson--Lindenstrauss (JL) projection lowers $d$ to $m\ll d$ while preserving pairwise distances, yet a random JL matrix must be committed and its sampling proved inside the circuit, which is costly and a leakage risk. We construct a public deterministic projection from the standardized orbit of a Pisot $β$-transformation, analyzed through the spectral gap of the $β$-map, the geometric decay of its correlations, rather than equidistribution. We prove that the induced squared-norm estimator is unbiased up to a term decaying geometrically with a sampling gap, and that its variance is $V_0/m$ with a constant $V_0$ that is dimension-free in experiment and, under one stated concentration hypothesis, in theory. A single public seed preserving all pairwise centroid distances therefore exists and is found by search. Against six standard projections, including the chaotic-sequence matrix of Yu \emph{et al.}, the construction matches statistical quality to within measurement noise, and it is the only one simultaneously free of in-circuit randomness and exactly reproducible in a fixed finite field at a per-step cost $\log_2β$ rather than $2^{k}$.

</details>


### [350] [Counterexamples to the Markovity Conjecture for the Two-Receiver Broadcast Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13170)
*Yanxiao Liu,Mian Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过两个反例反驳了Gohari、Liu和Nair在ISIT 2025上提出的Markovity猜想。该猜想涉及Marton内界对偶泛函的最优解的马尔可夫结构，若成立将大幅简化Marton内界的计算。两个反例均为三进制输入广播信道，具有严格正转移概率，并使用相同的非矩形2×2辅助结构。作者展示了显式的非马尔可夫构造，其目标值严格优于任何满足该猜想马尔可夫结构的构造，两个反例均借助GPT-5.6 Sol获得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Markovity猜想的正确性。该猜想是关于Marton内界对偶泛函优化器结构的核心假设，若成立可极大简化Marton内界的评估计算。通过寻找反例来检验该猜想的普适性，对广播信道容量区域理论具有重要意义。

Method: 构造三进制输入广播信道反例，采用非矩形2×2辅助结构，利用严格正转移概率。通过计算比较非马尔可夫构造与满足马尔可夫猜想结构下的目标值，证明前者严格更大。反例的寻找借助GPT-5.6 Sol辅助完成。

Result: 成功构造了两个反例，证明存在非马尔可夫构造的目标值严格优于任何满足马尔可夫结构约束的构造，从而推翻Markovity猜想。

Conclusion: Markovity猜想不成立。Marton内界的评估不能依赖该猜测的马尔可夫最优结构简化，需要寻找其他途径。该结果对广播信道容量区域的紧致化计算提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: We present two counterexamples to the \emph{Markovity Conjecture} of Gohari, Liu and Nair (ISIT 2025), which is a structural conjecture concerning the optimizers of the dual functional associated with Marton's inner bound and, if true, would greatly simplify the evaluation of Marton's inner bound. Both counterexamples are ternary-input broadcast channels with strictly positive transition probabilities and use the same nonrectangular $2\times2$ auxiliary structure. In each case, we exhibit an explicit non-Markov construction whose objective value is rigorously larger than that achievable by any construction satisfying the conjectured Markov structure. Both examples are obtained with the assistance of GPT-5.6 Sol and disprove the Markovity Conjecture.

</details>


### [351] [Age of Incorrect Information for Pull-Based State Estimation of General Markov Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13248)
*Marco Zanni,Mohamad Assaad,Touraj Soleymani*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意多状态马尔可夫源的拉式远程状态估计问题，同时考虑信息的新鲜度和正确性，将问题建模为基于年龄错误信息（AoII）的联合源-AoII信念马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。作者证明了每个可达信念可由最后成功观测的源状态及经过的时间槽数表示；截断无成功持续时间并给出误差界；对可靠链路给出查表式最优策略，对不可靠链路提出持续策略；引入混合估计器降低内存需求；并将框架扩展到多源调度，提出基于插值的近似Whittle索引策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程状态估计中，传统指标（如AoI）仅反映信息新鲜度，未考虑信息正确性。AoII指标同时兼顾新鲜度和正确性，但在任意多状态马尔可夫源下其优化问题具有高维信念状态，计算复杂度高，缺乏结构化的解析结果。本文旨在通过挖掘模型信息结构来解决这一难题。

Method: 1) 将AoII优化问题建模为联合源-AoII信念MDP，采用MAP估计；2) 利用信息结构证明任一可达信念可用（最后成功观测状态，经过时间槽数）二元组表示，大幅降低状态空间；3) 对截断参数给出显式误差界和选取准则；4) 对可靠链路推导查表式最优策略，对不可靠链路提出持续策略；5) 提出混合估计器（早期平稳切换）以降低内存；6) 扩展到多源场景，建模为Restless多臂赌博机，证明可索引性充分条件并提出插值式近似Whittle索引策略。

Result: 证明了信念状态的可降维表示性质；给出截断误差界和截断参数选择准则；证明MAP估计在有限时间槽后稳定；建立了多源调度问题的可索引性充分条件。数值实验验证了单源最优策略的结构以及所提启发式策略（持续策略、混合估计器、Whittle索引策略）接近最优解且大幅降低计算开销。

Conclusion: 本文通过利用信念状态的信息结构，将AoII优化问题从高维信念状态简化为二维表示，并提出了多种计算高效的近似策略，在接近最优性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度。所提出的框架适用于单源和多源远程状态估计场景，为AoII感知的拉式状态估计提供了系统性的理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: We study pull-based remote state estimation of an arbitrary, multi-state Markov source while accounting for both freshness and correctness attributes of information. To that end, we formulate a discounted optimization problem in terms of the age of incorrect information (AoII), and express it as a joint source-AoII belief Markov decision process (MDP) under maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation. We then exploit the information structure of the model and prove that every reachable belief is represented by the last successfully observed source state and the number of time slots elapsed since that observation. For numerical computation, we truncate the elapsed no-success duration at a finite level and derive an explicit error bound and a criterion for selecting the truncation parameter. For reliable links, we show that an optimal policy can be represented by a look-up table of waiting times. For unreliable links, we propose a persistent policy and derive computable performance bounds. We also show that the MAP estimate stabilizes after a finite number of time slots. To further reduce memory requirements, we introduce a hybrid estimator with an early stationary switch and derive a computable bound on the resulting difference in performance. Finally, we extend the framework to multiple sources, formulate the scheduling problem as a restless multi-armed bandit, establish a sufficient condition for indexability, and develop an approximate Whittle index policy based on interpolation. Our numerical results illustrate the structure of the optimal single-source policy, evaluate the performance of the multi-source policies, and verify that the proposed heuristic policies closely approach the optimal solution while substantially reducing computational efforts.

</details>


### [352] [Impedance-Aware Zonal Port Activation for Fluid Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13249)
*Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出阻抗感知的分区端口激活（IA-ZPA）方法，将有限端口流体天线阵列（FAA）激活建模为速率-孔径-可行性的联合优化问题，在精确射频链预算约束下实现高约束速率与低旁瓣电平的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAA激活需同时决定预编码的有效多用户信道和稀疏物理孔径。信道导向的选择虽能利用瞬时CSI但会侵蚀孔径质量，几何导向的选择则无法适应瞬时CSI，二者均存在局限。

Method: IA-ZPA将紧致CSI条件化的端口评分与棋盘可行性投影及推理时互阻抗感知选择相结合。学习型评分器对端口排序，确定性规则固定有效孔径，独立的电流域RZF后端评估源驱动可行性。

Result: 在诱导EMF协议下，IA-ZPA在满足预设平均PSLL目标的所有方法中取得最大约束速率，且决策时间显著低于贪心选择方法。

Conclusion: IA-ZPA通过耦合学习型端口评分与确定性可行性规则，实现了信道自适应的同时保持孔径质量，在速率和决策效率上均优于现有方案。

Abstract: Fluid antenna array (FAA) activation jointly determines the effective multi-user channel for precoding and the sparse physical aperture. Channel-oriented selection can concentrate high-gain ports and erode aperture quality, whereas geometry-oriented selection does not adapt to instantaneous channel state information (CSI). This paper formulates finite-port FAA activation as a rate--aperture--feasibility problem under an exact RF-chain budget. We propose impedance-aware zonal port activation (IA-ZPA), which couples compact CSI-conditioned port scoring with a checkerboard feasibility projection and inference-time mutual-impedance-aware selection. The learned scorer ranks ports, while the deterministic rule fixes the active aperture; a separate current-domain RZF backend then evaluates source-drive feasibility. Under a common induced-EMF protocol, IA-ZPA attains the largest constrained rate among the methods satisfying the prescribed mean-PSLL target with a substantially lower decision time than greedy selection.

</details>


### [353] [Resource-efficient Semantic Coding Schemes with Manifold-constrained Hyper-connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13253)
*Jingwen Fu,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种基于流形约束超连接（mHC）与熵瓶颈（EB）的编码方案，用于资源高效的语义通信和任务导向通信，在不增加信道使用次数和计算开销的前提下提升表示多样性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的语义通信收发器常通过增大编码器规模或使用更高维的信道特征来提高可靠性，但这会显著增加计算复杂度和信道占用次数。因此，需要显式的码率控制来平衡性能与传输资源（如带宽和功率）。

Method: 提出mHC语义编码器，采用多条残差流并通过双重随机（DS）混合矩阵约束其交互，替代单一的残差路径，以提升表示多样性和训练稳定性且几乎不增加参数和浮点开销；同时引入熵瓶颈（EB）对信道特征进行量化并估计熵编码速率，实现端到端的率失真/任务优化，受带宽和发射功率约束。理论上证明DS约束的流混合不会增加传输特征的微分熵，即不增加理想EB编码长度。

Result: 在AWGN、瑞利衰落、莱斯衰落和不完美CSI场景下的SemCom与TOC实验中，所提方案在语义/任务性能、通信鲁棒性和收敛稳定性上均优于残差基线和未约束HC基线，且无需额外的信道使用。

Conclusion: mHC+EB方案在保持通信资源开销不变的前提下，通过显式码率控制与多流DS约束结构，实现了更优的语义/任务性能与鲁棒性，为资源高效语义通信提供了有效设计范式。

Abstract: Semantic communication (SemCom) and task-oriented communication (TOC) can reduce wireless resource consumption by focusing on transmitting semantic or task-relevant information instead of raw messages. In practice, a main challenge is to make transmitting information robust to channel noise and fading while keeping it compact. Existing learning-based transceivers often improve reliability by using larger encoders or higher-dimensional channel features, which increase computation complexity and channel uses. Therefore, optimized system design needs explicit rate control to balance performance and transmitting resources e.g., bandwidth and power. For this purpose, we propose a manifold-constrained hyper-connection (mHC) coding scheme with an entropy bottleneck (EB) for resource-efficient SemCom and TOC over wireless channels. Instead of using a single residual path of existing encoders, the proposed mHC-based semantic encoder applies multiple residual streams and constrains their interaction by doubly stochastic (DS) mixing matrices. The new structure improves representation diversity and training stability with negligible parameter and floating-point overhead. The EB quantizes the channel features and estimates the entropy-coded rate, enabling end-to-end rate--distortion/task optimization under bandwidth and transmit-power constraints. We further show that DS-constrained stream mixing does not increase the differential entropy of the transmitted features. This implies no increase in the ideal EB coding length. Experiments on SemCom and TOC under additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN), Rayleigh fading, Rician fading, and imperfect channel state information (CSI) show that the proposed scheme improves semantic/task performance, communication robustness, and convergence stability over residual and unconstrained HC baselines, while requiring no additional channel uses.

</details>


### [354] [EM-Guided Graph Learning for Fluid Antenna Beamforming under Current-Domain Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13254)
*Yuanhui Wu,Hao Jiang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究流体天线阵列(FAA)在下行多用户波束成形中的端口放置与电流域传输联合优化问题，提出一种电磁引导的图网络方法，在通信性能与电磁可行性之间实现可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧凑孔径中，通道驱动的端口放置可能导致端口聚集、互耦增强、辐射条件恶化、源电压需求增加以及电流负载不均等问题，现有方法缺乏对电磁可行性的充分考量。

Method: 提出电磁引导的图网络，从通道观测中预测端口布局，并利用几何与互阻抗信息进行细化；训练目标同时考虑通信性能与电磁可行性，所有方法采用统一的评估流程。

Result: 在统一的可行性标准下，所提方法能够在通信速率、电流负载和配置延迟之间提供可控的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将电磁约束纳入端口放置与传输联合优化的设计框架是可行且有效的，为流体天线系统的实际部署提供了更为全面的优化视角。

Abstract: Fluid antenna arrays (FAAs) reconfigure a finite set of radiating ports within a prescribed aperture. In compact apertures, however, channel-driven placement may cluster ports, strengthen mutual coupling, degrade radiation conditioning, increase source-voltage demand, and produce uneven current loading. This paper studies downlink multi-user beamforming with jointly optimized port placement and current-domain transmission. An electromagnetic-guided graph network predicts port layouts from channel observations and refines them using geometric and mutual-impedance information. The training objective jointly considers communication performance and electromagnetic feasibility, while a common evaluation procedure is applied to all methods. The results show that, under a common feasibility standard, the proposed method provides a controllable tradeoff among communication rate, current loading, and configuration latency.

</details>


### [355] [MiLAC-Aided Beamforming for MIMO Over-the-Air Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13353)
*Yaru Wang,Deyou Zhang,Qingchao Li,Jun Liu,Chuang Shi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于微波线性模拟计算机（MiLAC）辅助波束赋形的MIMO空中计算（AirComp）系统，通过联合优化发射端数字预编码矩阵和接收端MiLAC聚合矩阵来最小化均方误差（MSE），该方案在减少射频链数量的同时接近全数字波束赋形的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AirComp通过空中计算实现低时延无线数据聚合，但其精度受限于衰落信道下的信号对准误差和接收噪声。全数字波束赋形虽然能提高MIMO AirComp系统的聚合精度，但需要每根天线配备一条射频链，硬件开销过大。因此需要一种既能保证精度又降低硬件复杂度的方案。

Method: 在无损且互易的MiLAC模型下，联合优化发射端数字预编码矩阵和接收端MiLAC聚合矩阵以最小化MSE。采用交替优化算法：预编码矩阵通过Karush-Kuhn-Tucker条件和二分法最优更新，接收端聚合矩阵子问题通过投影梯度下降全局求解。

Result: 数值结果验证了算法的收敛性，并表明MiLAC辅助波束赋形在显著减少射频链数量的条件下能接近全数字波束赋形的MSE性能，且在相同射频链预算下优于基于移相器的混合波束赋形方案。

Conclusion: MiLAC辅助波束赋形是一种有效的折中方案，能够在维持接近全数字波束赋形的聚合精度同时大幅降低射频链硬件开销，为高频段MIMO AirComp系统提供了实用的硬件高效实现途径。

Abstract: Over-the-air computation (AirComp) enables low-latency wireless data aggregation, but its accuracy is limited by imperfect signal alignment over fading channels and receiver noise. Fully digital beamforming improves aggregation accuracy in multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) AirComp systems but requires one radio-frequency (RF) chain per antenna. To reduce this hardware burden, we investigate microwave linear analog computer (MiLAC)-aided beamforming for MIMO AirComp. Under a lossless and reciprocal MiLAC model, we jointly optimize the transmit digital precoding matrices and the receive-side MiLAC aggregation matrix to minimize the mean squared error (MSE). An alternating optimization algorithm is developed, in which the precoding matrices are optimally updated using the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions and bisection, while the resulting convex aggregation matrix subproblem is solved globally using projected gradient descent. Numerical results verify the algorithm's convergence and demonstrate that MiLAC-aided beamforming approaches the MSE performance of fully digital beamforming with substantially fewer RF chains and outperforms phase-shifter-based hybrid beamforming under the same RF-chain budget.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [356] [A Repeated-Game Framework for Incentives in Decentralized Infrastructure Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12576)
*Mustafa Qazi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个重复动态激励框架，用于刻画去中心化物理基础设施网络（DePIN）中"合规"（即全力诚实提供服务）的激励相容条件。核心贡献是"威慑比率"Γ——偏离行为的私人收益与其边际检测概率的最坏情况比率，并据此推导出合规在逐层声誉等级上序贯激励相容的充要条件，进而构建协议设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DePIN中的质量控制本质上是一个协议与各提供商之间的重复道德风险问题，但现有文献缺乏一个统一的动态激励框架来刻画何种条件下合规（full-effort honest service provision）是可激励相容的。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，为DePIN协议设计提供严格的基础。

Method: 将DePIN中的质量控制建模为协议与每个提供商之间的重复道德风险问题，通过两种机制执行合规：(1) 抵押质押金的削减（slashing）；(2) 声誉阶梯上降级至试用层的折现威胁。引入核心概念"威慑比率"Γ，即偏离行为私人收益与边际检测概率的最坏情况比率。基于此推导合规序贯激励相容的充要条件，并将该条件转化为协议设计优化问题，映射服务原语到质押要求、奖励计划、试用规则与审计频率。

Result: (1) 若任何有利可图的偏离行为不增加相对合规的失败概率，则二元公开结果协议无法威慑该偏离；(2) 当所有有利偏离具有正的检测差距且没有弱更昂贵的行为更不可能失败时，合规在每个声誉等级上序贯激励相容当且仅当立即削减加上折现声誉损失至少等于该等级上的Γ；(3) 据此提出一个协议设计问题，将服务原语映射到具体的质押、奖励、试用与审计参数。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为DePIN协议如何设计激励机制以确保合规提供了充要条件与可操作的设计工具，核心洞见是：仅凭二元公开结果协议不足以威慑那些不改变失败概率的偏离行为；有效的威慑需要"立即削减+折现声誉损失"的组合强度至少覆盖每个声誉等级上的威慑比率Γ。

Abstract: We introduce a repeated dynamic incentive framework for characterizing when "compliance", or full-effort honest service provision, is incentive compatible in Decentralized Physical Infrastructure Networks (DePIN). We model quality control in this setting as a repeated moral-hazard problem between the protocol and each provider, where compliance is enforced by both slashing posted collateral and the discounted threat of demotion to probation tiers on a reputation ladder. Our main contribution is the "deterrence ratio" $Γ$, the worst-case ratio of a deviation's private gain to its marginal probability of detection. We find that if any profitable deviation does not increase the fail probability relative to compliance, then binary public-outcome protocols cannot deter it. When all profitable deviations have positive detection gaps and no weakly costlier action is less likely to fail, compliance is sequentially incentive compatible if and only if immediate slashing plus discounted reputation loss is at least $Γ$ at every reputation tier. We use this condition to formulate a protocol design problem mapping service primitives to stake requirements, reward schedules, probation rules, and audit frequency.

</details>


### [357] [Error-Aware Reverse Auction Mechanism for Large Language Model Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12719)
*Haolong Chen,Zhengyuan Xin,Liang Zhang,Lei Xue,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于市场的路由范式——通过反向拍卖（reverse auction）让LLM提供商以自预测的成功概率和执行成本进行竞标，取代传统的集中式预测中心。作者设计了误差感知的反向拍卖机制（EA-RAM），显式建模提供商预测与中心评估中的双重误差，并证明了该机制在贝叶斯激励相容性、个体理性、中心理性充分条件及社会福利损失界等方面的理论性质，实验验证其在仿真和真实基准上优于集中式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM路由依赖集中式任务中心预测各模型性能，存在两个核心问题：一是信息-风险错配（中心缺乏各模型的专有信息却承担预测责任），二是随着模型池规模扩大出现可扩展性瓶颈。因此需要一种去中心化、可扩展且能平衡质量与成本的路由范式。

Method: 提出反向拍卖路由机制：由LLM提供商提交自预测成功概率与执行成本的竞标，通过反向拍卖实现路由决策。在此基础上引入EA-RAM机制，显式建模提供商预测噪声与中心评估噪声构成的"双重误差"（Dual Error），并给出相应的理论分析框架，包括激励相容性证明、个体理性条件、中心理性充分条件以及福利损失上界推导，同时分析误差的鲁棒性效应（符号相消、饱和稳定、噪声平滑）。

Result: 理论层面证明了EA-RAM在双重误差下满足贝叶斯激励相容与个体理性，建立了中心理性的充分条件和显式福利损失界；识别出三类鲁棒性效应：异号误差可相互抵消、类logistic的尾部消失链接函数通过饱和稳定明确案例、额外噪声使信念图平滑从而降低边际操纵收益。实验层面，在仿真和真实基准上EA-RAM对双重误差鲁棒，且比集中式基线取得更优的成本-性能Pareto前沿，提供商贡献本地信息时增益进一步扩大。

Conclusion: EA-RAM作为首个考虑预测与评估双重误差的市场化LLM路由机制，将"事前预测"从中心转移到提供商端，不仅解决了集中式路由的信息错配与扩展瓶颈，还在理论与实践上证明了其鲁棒性和有效性。未来提供商本地信息的利用可进一步提升系统整体性能。

Abstract: Routing each query to a cost-effective large language model (LLM) is critical for balancing quality and cost, yet most routers rely on a centralized task center to predict model performance, creating an information-risk mismatch and a scalability bottleneck as the model pool grows. We propose a market-based routing paradigm that shifts ex-ante prediction to LLM providers via a reverse auction, where providers bid with self-predicted success probabilities and execution costs. To account for inherently noisy provider predictions and center evaluations, we introduce the \textit{\textbf{E}rror-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}everse \textbf{A}uction \textbf{M}echanism} (EA-RAM), which explicitly models this inherent Dual Error. We prove that EA-RAM is Bayesian incentive compatible and individually rational under the Dual Error, establish sufficient conditions for center rationality, and derive an explicit welfare-loss bound. We further identify robustness effects: opposite-signed errors can cancel, vanishing-tail link functions (e.g., logistic) stabilize clear-cut cases via saturation, and extra noise smooths belief maps, reducing the gains from marginal manipulation. Experiments on simulations and real-world benchmarks show that EA-RAM is robust to the Dual Error and achieves a better cost--performance Pareto frontier than centralized baselines, with additional gains when providers contribute local information, validating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [358] [Representation in Peer Selection: A Liquid Democracy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13085)
*Davide Grossi,Grzegorz Lisowski,Georgios Papasotiropoulos*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一类新的受限偏好结构"液体档案"（liquid profiles），用于解决同行选择问题（peer selection）——即由一组同行从自身中选出固定规模委员会。每个选民指定一个受信任的代理人，并认可该代理人信任的所有人。文章建立了该结构与标准受限偏好档案的关系，研究了其计算性质，分析了新旧比例代表性公理在该域中的行为及相互关系，并提出受液体档案结构启发的新投票规则。研究发现该新域中可以实现特别强的比例代表性保障，有望应用于现实世界中的同行选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行选择问题（如委员会选举、评审团组建）在现实中有广泛应用，但标准的审批式偏好可能无法充分捕捉液体民主中信任的传递性。作者希望通过引入"液体档案"这一新的偏好域限制，在同行选择中实现更强的比例代表性保障，并为基于传递性委派的实际应用提供理论基础。

Method: 作者首先定义"液体档案"这一偏好域限制，其中每个投票者指定一个信任代理人，并批准所有该代理人信任的人。随后建立该域与标准受限偏好档案（如单峰偏好等）的联系，分析其计算复杂性。接着检验多种既有和新提出的比例公理在此域中的可满足性及相互关系，并设计受液体档案结构启发的新投票规则，分析其公理性满足情况。

Result: 研究发现，液体档案域中可实现特别强的比例代表性保障。多种比例公理在此域中表现出新的行为模式，新设计的投票规则能够满足此前在一般域中不可能实现的比例保证，表明该域结构对实现强比例性具有天然优势。

Conclusion: 液体档案为同行选择问题提供了一个具有理论优美性和实际可行性的新偏好域，通过信任传递实现强比例代表性。该工作为液体民主与同行选择之间的桥梁提供了理论支撑，并展示了实际应用的潜力。

Abstract: We study the peer selection problem in which a group of peers must select a fixed-size committee from among themselves. To this end, we introduce a class of approval-based preference profiles, inspired by delegation graphs in liquid democracy and the transitivity of trust therein, which we call ``liquid profiles''. Under this new domain restriction, each voter designates a single trusted agent and, in turn, approves all those trusted by that individual. After establishing relationships to standard restricted preference profiles and examining computational properties of liquid profiles, we focus on understanding how novel and established proportionality axioms behave in this domain and how they relate to one another. Then, we study their satisfaction under various voting rules, including new ones that are directly inspired by the structure of liquid profiles. Our findings reveal that particularly strong proportionality guarantees are attainable in this new domain, showing promise for applications to real-world peer selection problems via transitive delegations.

</details>


### [359] [Power in Liquid Democracy: A Network Centrality Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13188)
*Davide Grossi,Andreas Nitsche,Georgios Papasotiropoulos,Oskar Skibski,Piotr Skowron,Tomasz Wąs*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算上可行的框架，用于衡量支持传递性和可暂停委派的集体决策平台（即流动民主）中选民的权力，采用随机游走衰减中心性（Random Walk Decay centrality）来捕捉影响力在委派网络中的传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流动民主平台中，选民的权力分配是核心问题，但现有框架（如PageRank）在捕捉委派网络中的影响力传播方面存在不足，且缺乏计算上可行且具有公理化基础的度量方法。

Method: 采用随机游走衰减中心性（Random Walk Decay centrality）度量权力，给出其在此情境下的直觉合理性论证及其相对于PageRank等替代方法的优势，并在权力度量类别中推导出自然的公理化刻画；同时提出选取代表性关键参与者榜单（slates）的方法，并从公理与算法两个角度进行研究。

Result: 建立了具有公理化基础的权力度量框架，该框架计算可行且在流动民主情境下优于现有替代方法；提出的代表性榜单选择方法也在公理与算法层面得到研究验证。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为理解流动民主中权力分配提供了坚实的理论基础，也具备扩展为分析此类平台权力分布的实用工具的潜力。

Abstract: This paper develops a computationally tractable framework for measuring voters' power in collective decision-making platforms that support transitive and suspendable delegations---commonly known as liquid democracy. We employ Random Walk Decay centrality to capture how influence propagates through delegation networks, argue for its intuitive appeal in this context and its advantages over alternatives such as PageRank, and derive a natural axiomatic characterization within the class of power metrics. Moreover, we conceptualize how the framework can be extended into a practical tool for analyzing power distributions in such platforms, and propose and study, both axiomatically and algorithmically, methods for selecting representative slates of influential participants.

</details>


### [360] [Keep, Customize, or Exit: Default Design and Token Pricing in LLM Reasoning Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13315)
*Ahmet Bugra Gundogan,Yigit Turkmen,Melih Bastopcu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型（LLM）服务中的定价与推理令牌分配问题，将该交互建模为Stackelberg博弈，推导出用户最优定制分配闭式解，刻画了提供商最优默认规则的三段式特征，并证明均衡存在性，最后通过实验验证了模型假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM服务中，提供商设定每令牌价格和默认推理令牌分配，用户可选择接受默认、自定义或退出。更大的推理分配可提升准确性，但增加令牌成本和延迟。这种交互中的定价与默认分配策略尚未被系统研究，需要一个理论框架来刻画双方的最优决策与均衡。

Method: 将提供商与用户的交互建模为Stackelberg博弈（提供商为领导者，用户为跟随者）。推导用户唯一最优定制分配的闭式解；证明可接受的默认分配集合为空集或紧区间；通过三段式规则刻画提供商最优默认；将均衡计算约简为一维价格优化；证明均衡存在性。使用两个紧凑开放权重推理模型在五个数学和科学基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 默认分配仅在用户重视避免定制的便利性时才会影响实际执行的推理分配；否则，所有服务提供结果都实现用户的最优定制分配。实验支持准确性-令牌模型，并展示了模型和任务特征如何决定均衡价格、默认分配和推理分配。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM服务的定价与推理分配提供了完整的博弈论分析框架，揭示了默认分配的作用边界（仅在用户重视便利性时生效），并为提供商在不同模型和任务特征下的均衡策略提供了理论指导与实证验证。

Abstract: We study a large language model (LLM) service in which a provider chooses a per-token price and a default reasoning-token allocation, while a user may accept the default, customize the allocation, or exit. Larger allocations can improve accuracy but increase token cost and latency. We model this interaction as a Stackelberg game and derive the user's unique optimal customized allocation in closed form. For any price, the acceptable defaults form either an empty set or a compact interval. We characterize the provider's optimal default through a three-regime rule, reduce equilibrium computation to a one-dimensional price optimization, and prove the existence of the equilibrium. We further show that defaults affect the implemented reasoning allocation only when users value the convenience of avoiding customization; otherwise, every service-providing outcome implements the user's optimal customized allocation. Experiments with two compact open-weight reasoning models on five mathematics and science benchmarks support the accuracy-token model and show how model and task characteristics determine equilibrium prices, defaults, and reasoning allocations.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [361] [Matrix-Driven Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) Surfaces Structural Framework: Continuity Limitations, Computer Graphics Algorithms, and Software Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12697)
*Hakan Üstünel*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种四次Overhauser（QOVR）曲面生成框架，通过可变参数的四次架构实现正交坐标轴上的精确参数隔离，满足C^0和C^1连续性条件，实现局部形状修改而不影响全局边界，验证了局部位移效应的指数衰减特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统曲面生成方法难以同时满足边界对齐和局部形状修改的需求，通常局部调整会引发全局曲面传播效应（如曲率传播、边缘分离或波浪伪影），因此需要一种能够解耦局部与全局影响的曲面构造框架。

Method: 采用可变参数的四次（fourth degree）新架构，在正交坐标轴间实现精确参数隔离；通过方向性样条混合函数与对称空间控制矩阵完成解析管线，内部节点向量变化允许局部曲面调整同时保持全局边界的绝对位置不变性。

Result: 计算验证表明该框架满足明确的C^0位置闭合和C^1切向连续性，不触发全局曲率传播、边缘连接分离或波浪状伪影；但C^2曲率连续性未保持。三维网格模型和颜色图可视化证明空间强度场严格凝聚于被修改元素的紧邻区域，位移效应随与控制点的距离呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 该显式解耦特性保持了相邻几何补片之间的结构对称性和边界不变性，满足制造与逆向工程的密封标准，为CAGD领域提供了一种兼顾局部控制精度与全局稳定性的曲面设计解决方案。

Abstract: This study introduces the spatial and analytical construction of the Quartic Overhauser (QOVR) surface generation framework designed to resolve boundary alignment and localized shape modification constraints. This framework implements a variable parameter fourth degree novel architecture to achieve exact parameter isolation across orthogonal coordinate axes. The analytical pipeline integrates directional spline blending functions with symmetric spatial control matrices, ensuring that internal knot vector variations allow localized surface adjustments while preserving the absolute positional invariance of global edge boundaries. Computational verification confirms that while the current formulation satisfies explicit C^0 positional closure and C^1 tangent continuity conditions across the internal and boundary interfaces without triggering global curvature propagation, edge joint separation, or wave-like artifacts, C^2 curvature continuity is not maintained. As demonstrated by three-dimensional mesh models and colormap visualizations, the spatial intensity fields condense strictly within the immediate neighborhood of the modified element, verifying that the displacement effect decreases exponentially as the distance from the perturbed control points increases. This explicit decoupling preserves structural symmetry and boundary invariance across adjacent geometric patches, satisfying manufacturing and reverse engineering sealing criteria.
  Keywords: Quartic Overhauser surface; computer aided geometric design (CAGD); local shape control; geometric continuity; symmetric tensor products; computer graphics algorithms; software implementation framework.

</details>


### [362] [Blue Noise as a Lattice Gibbs Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13446)
*Zhuoran Yi*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将蓝噪声采样建模为具有成对排斥相互作用的二元晶格占用上的Gibbs分布，通过反向耦合Markov链（CFTP）进行采样，实现可并行、可扩展且具有有界误差保证的蓝噪声生成。该方法支持分块生成，块间无需通信且输出与全域生成逐位一致，内存开销由块大小而非输出大小决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蓝噪声生成方法存在根本性的割裂：优化和传输方法通过耦合所有采样点来产生高质量点集，但无法随输出规模扩展；程序式或基于瓦片（tile）的采样器是局部的，但输出仅隐式定义，缺乏统计上的显式模型保证。作者希望弥合统计建模与可扩展生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: 将蓝噪声生成形式化为从带有成对排斥相互作用的Gibbs分布中采样二元晶格占用模式。由于能量对样本对求和，远距离交互可以以有界分布变化被截断，从而得到有界度的马尔可夫随机场。采样采用反向耦合马尔可夫链（Coupling Towards The Past, CFTP），从目标状态反向追踪并在固定深度处截断，从而限制计算成本和样本依赖区域。独立的瓦片带上足够的光晕后，与任何更大域上同一区域生成的结果逐位一致，瓦片间无需通信。密度、排斥强度、相互作用尺度及核硬度均为分布的可调参数。

Result: 实验验证了模型、采样器和保证三个方面：集成体再现了标准的蓝噪声频谱，参数变化时频谱连续过渡；采样器的工作量和内存与理论预测一致；分块输出与全域生成逐位一致。展示了14K分辨率的自适应点画（stippling）以及多类扩展，而现有方法在此场景需要与输出成比例的内存。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一的概率模型框架同时实现了蓝噪声的显式统计建模与可扩展的按块生成，提供有证明的误差界，并通过精度与成本的参数化权衡取代了不同算法之间的切换，为大规模蓝噪声采样提供了新范式。

Abstract: Blue-noise sampling is widely used in computer graphics, but existing methods separate statistical modeling from scalable generation. Optimization and transport methods produce high-quality point sets by coupling all samples together. Procedural and tile-based samplers are local, but define their output only implicitly.
  We formulate blue-noise generation as sampling from a Gibbs distribution over binary lattice occupancies with pairwise repulsive interactions. Density, repulsion strength, interaction scale, and kernel hardness are parameters of this distribution. Because the energy sums over pairs, distant interactions can be dropped with a bounded change to the distribution, leaving a Markov random field of bounded degree.
  To sample it, we trace the Markov chain backward from the state we want, following Coupling Towards The Past, and cut the trace at a fixed depth. This bounds the cost, and it bounds the region each sample depends on. A tile generated on its own, with a sufficient halo, is then bit-identical to the same region generated on any larger domain, in any order and with no communication between tiles. Memory is set by the tile size, not by the output size, and accuracy is traded against cost through parameters with a proven error bound rather than by switching algorithms.
  We validate the model, the sampler, and these guarantees separately. The ensemble reproduces standard blue-noise spectra and moves continuously between them as its parameters vary. The sampler matches its predicted work and memory. Tiled output is verified bit-identical to full-domain generation. We demonstrate adaptive stippling at 14K, where existing methods need memory proportional to the output, along with multi-class extensions.

</details>
